1988void initServerClientMemUsageBuckets(void) {
1989    if (server.client_mem_usage_buckets)
1990        return;
1991    server.client_mem_usage_buckets = zmalloc(sizeof(clientMemUsageBucket)*CLIENT_MEM_USAGE_BUCKETS);
1992    for (int j = 0; j < CLIENT_MEM_USAGE_BUCKETS; j++) {
1993        server.client_mem_usage_buckets[j].mem_usage_sum = 0;
1994        server.client_mem_usage_buckets[j].clients = listCreate();
1995    }
1996}
1997
1998void freeServerClientMemUsageBuckets(void) {
1999    if (!server.client_mem_usage_buckets)
2000        return;
2001    for (int j = 0; j < CLIENT_MEM_USAGE_BUCKETS; j++)
2002        listRelease(server.client_mem_usage_buckets[j].clients);
2003    zfree(server.client_mem_usage_buckets);
2004    server.client_mem_usage_buckets = NULL;
2005}
2006
2007void initServerConfig(void) {
2008    int j;
2009    char *default_bindaddr[CONFIG_DEFAULT_BINDADDR_COUNT] = CONFIG_DEFAULT_BINDADDR;
2010
2011    initConfigValues();
2012    updateCachedTime(1);
2013    server.cmd_time_snapshot = server.mstime;
2014    getRandomHexChars(server.runid,CONFIG_RUN_ID_SIZE);
2015    server.runid[CONFIG_RUN_ID_SIZE] = '\0';
2016    changeReplicationId();
2017    clearReplicationId2();
2018    server.hz = CONFIG_DEFAULT_HZ; /* Initialize it ASAP, even if it may get
2019                                      updated later after loading the config.
2020                                      This value may be used before the server
2021                                      is initialized. */
2022    server.timezone = getTimeZone(); /* Initialized by tzset(). */
2023    server.configfile = NULL;
2024    server.executable = NULL;
2025    server.arch_bits = (sizeof(long) == 8) ? 64 : 32;
2026    server.bindaddr_count = CONFIG_DEFAULT_BINDADDR_COUNT;
2027    for (j = 0; j < CONFIG_DEFAULT_BINDADDR_COUNT; j++)
2028        server.bindaddr[j] = zstrdup(default_bindaddr[j]);
2029    memset(server.listeners, 0x00, sizeof(server.listeners));
2030    server.active_expire_enabled = 1;
2031    server.lazy_expire_disabled = 0;
2032    server.skip_checksum_validation = 0;
2033    server.loading = 0;
2034    server.async_loading = 0;
2035    server.loading_rdb_used_mem = 0;
2036    server.aof_state = AOF_OFF;
2037    server.aof_rewrite_base_size = 0;
2038    server.aof_rewrite_scheduled = 0;
2039    server.aof_flush_sleep = 0;
2040    server.aof_last_fsync = time(NULL);
2041    server.aof_cur_timestamp = 0;
2042    atomicSet(server.aof_bio_fsync_status,C_OK);
2043    server.aof_rewrite_time_last = -1;
2044    server.aof_rewrite_time_start = -1;
2045    server.aof_lastbgrewrite_status = C_OK;
2046    server.aof_delayed_fsync = 0;
2047    server.aof_fd = -1;
2048    server.aof_selected_db = -1; /* Make sure the first time will not match */
2049    server.aof_flush_postponed_start = 0;
2050    server.aof_last_incr_size = 0;
2051    server.aof_last_incr_fsync_offset = 0;
2052    server.active_defrag_running = 0;
2053    server.notify_keyspace_events = 0;
2054    server.blocked_clients = 0;
2055    memset(server.blocked_clients_by_type,0,
2056           sizeof(server.blocked_clients_by_type));
2057    server.shutdown_asap = 0;
2058    server.shutdown_flags = 0;
2059    server.shutdown_mstime = 0;
2060    server.cluster_module_flags = CLUSTER_MODULE_FLAG_NONE;
2061    server.migrate_cached_sockets = dictCreate(&migrateCacheDictType);
2062    server.next_client_id = 1; /* Client IDs, start from 1 .*/
2063    server.page_size = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE);
2064    server.pause_cron = 0;
2065
2066    server.latency_tracking_info_percentiles_len = 3;
2067    server.latency_tracking_info_percentiles = zmalloc(sizeof(double)*(server.latency_tracking_info_percentiles_len));
2068    server.latency_tracking_info_percentiles[0] = 50.0;  /* p50 */
2069    server.latency_tracking_info_percentiles[1] = 99.0;  /* p99 */
2070    server.latency_tracking_info_percentiles[2] = 99.9;  /* p999 */
2071
2072    server.lruclock = getLRUClock();
2073    resetServerSaveParams();
2074
2075    appendServerSaveParams(60*60,1);  /* save after 1 hour and 1 change */
2076    appendServerSaveParams(300,100);  /* save after 5 minutes and 100 changes */
2077    appendServerSaveParams(60,10000); /* save after 1 minute and 10000 changes */
2078
2079    /* Replication related */
2080    server.masterhost = NULL;
2081    server.masterport = 6379;
2082    server.master = NULL;
2083    server.cached_master = NULL;
2084    server.master_initial_offset = -1;
2085    server.repl_state = REPL_STATE_NONE;
2086    server.repl_transfer_tmpfile = NULL;
2087    server.repl_transfer_fd = -1;
2088    server.repl_transfer_s = NULL;
2089    server.repl_syncio_timeout = CONFIG_REPL_SYNCIO_TIMEOUT;
2090    server.repl_down_since = 0; /* Never connected, repl is down since EVER. */
2091    server.master_repl_offset = 0;
2092    server.fsynced_reploff_pending = 0;
2093
2094    /* Replication partial resync backlog */
2095    server.repl_backlog = NULL;
2096    server.repl_no_slaves_since = time(NULL);
2097
2098    /* Failover related */
2099    server.failover_end_time = 0;
2100    server.force_failover = 0;
2101    server.target_replica_host = NULL;
2102    server.target_replica_port = 0;
2103    server.failover_state = NO_FAILOVER;
2104
2105    /* Client output buffer limits */
2106    for (j = 0; j < CLIENT_TYPE_OBUF_COUNT; j++)
2107        server.client_obuf_limits[j] = clientBufferLimitsDefaults[j];
2108
2109    /* Linux OOM Score config */
2110    for (j = 0; j < CONFIG_OOM_COUNT; j++)
2111        server.oom_score_adj_values[j] = configOOMScoreAdjValuesDefaults[j];
2112
2113    /* Double constants initialization */
2114    R_Zero = 0.0;
2115    R_PosInf = 1.0/R_Zero;
2116    R_NegInf = -1.0/R_Zero;
2117    R_Nan = R_Zero/R_Zero;
2118
2119    /* Command table -- we initialize it here as it is part of the
2120     * initial configuration, since command names may be changed via
2121     * redis.conf using the rename-command directive. */
2122    server.commands = dictCreate(&commandTableDictType);
2123    server.orig_commands = dictCreate(&commandTableDictType);
2124    populateCommandTable();
2125
2126    /* Debugging */
2127    server.watchdog_period = 0;
2128}
2129
2130extern char **environ;
2131
2132/* Restart the server, executing the same executable that started this
2133 * instance, with the same arguments and configuration file.
2134 *
2135 * The function is designed to directly call execve() so that the new
2136 * server instance will retain the PID of the previous one.
2137 *
2138 * The list of flags, that may be bitwise ORed together, alter the
2139 * behavior of this function:
2140 *
2141 * RESTART_SERVER_NONE              No flags.
2142 * RESTART_SERVER_GRACEFULLY        Do a proper shutdown before restarting.
2143 * RESTART_SERVER_CONFIG_REWRITE    Rewrite the config file before restarting.
2144 *
2145 * On success the function does not return, because the process turns into
2146 * a different process. On error C_ERR is returned. */
2147int restartServer(int flags, mstime_t delay) {
2148    int j;
2149
2150    /* Check if we still have accesses to the executable that started this
2151     * server instance. */
2152    if (access(server.executable,X_OK) == -1) {
2153        serverLog(LL_WARNING,"Can't restart: this process has no "
2154                             "permissions to execute %s", server.executable);
2155        return C_ERR;
2156    }
2157
2158    /* Config rewriting. */
2159    if (flags & RESTART_SERVER_CONFIG_REWRITE &&
2160        server.configfile &&
2161        rewriteConfig(server.configfile, 0) == -1)
2162    {
2163        serverLog(LL_WARNING,"Can't restart: configuration rewrite process "
2164                             "failed: %s", strerror(errno));
2165        return C_ERR;
2166    }
2167
2168    /* Perform a proper shutdown. We don't wait for lagging replicas though. */
2169    if (flags & RESTART_SERVER_GRACEFULLY &&
2170        prepareForShutdown(SHUTDOWN_NOW) != C_OK)
2171    {
2172        serverLog(LL_WARNING,"Can't restart: error preparing for shutdown");
2173        return C_ERR;
2174    }
2175
2176    /* Close all file descriptors, with the exception of stdin, stdout, stderr
2177     * which are useful if we restart a Redis server which is not daemonized. */
2178    for (j = 3; j < (int)server.maxclients + 1024; j++) {
2179        /* Test the descriptor validity before closing it, otherwise
2180         * Valgrind issues a warning on close(). */
2181        if (fcntl(j,F_GETFD) != -1) close(j);
2182    }
2183
2184    /* Execute the server with the original command line. */
2185    if (delay) usleep(delay*1000);
2186    zfree(server.exec_argv[0]);
2187    server.exec_argv[0] = zstrdup(server.executable);
2188    execve(server.executable,server.exec_argv,environ);
2189
2190    /* If an error occurred here, there is nothing we can do, but exit. */
2191    _exit(1);
2192
2193    return C_ERR; /* Never reached. */
2194}
2195
2196/* This function will configure the current process's oom_score_adj according
2197 * to user specified configuration. This is currently implemented on Linux
2198 * only.
2199 *
2200 * A process_class value of -1 implies OOM_CONFIG_MASTER or OOM_CONFIG_REPLICA,
2201 * depending on current role.
2202 */
2203int setOOMScoreAdj(int process_class) {
2204    if (process_class == -1)
2205        process_class = (server.masterhost ? CONFIG_OOM_REPLICA : CONFIG_OOM_MASTER);
2206
2207    serverAssert(process_class >= 0 && process_class < CONFIG_OOM_COUNT);
2208
2209#ifdef HAVE_PROC_OOM_SCORE_ADJ
2210    /* The following statics are used to indicate Redis has changed the process's oom score.
2211     * And to save the original score so we can restore it later if needed.
2212     * We need this so when we disabled oom-score-adj (also during configuration rollback
2213     * when another configuration parameter was invalid and causes a rollback after
2214     * applying a new oom-score) we can return to the oom-score value from before our
2215     * adjustments. */
2216    static int oom_score_adjusted_by_redis = 0;
2217    static int oom_score_adj_base = 0;
2218
2219    int fd;
2220    int val;
2221    char buf[64];
2222
2223    if (server.oom_score_adj != OOM_SCORE_ADJ_NO) {
2224        if (!oom_score_adjusted_by_redis) {
2225            oom_score_adjusted_by_redis = 1;
2226            /* Backup base value before enabling Redis control over oom score */
2227            fd = open("/proc/self/oom_score_adj", O_RDONLY);
2228            if (fd < 0 || read(fd, buf, sizeof(buf)) < 0) {
2229                serverLog(LL_WARNING, "Unable to read oom_score_adj: %s", strerror(errno));
2230                if (fd != -1) close(fd);
2231                return C_ERR;
2232            }
2233            oom_score_adj_base = atoi(buf);
2234            close(fd);
2235        }
2236
2237        val = server.oom_score_adj_values[process_class];
2238        if (server.oom_score_adj == OOM_SCORE_RELATIVE)
2239            val += oom_score_adj_base;
2240        if (val > 1000) val = 1000;
2241        if (val < -1000) val = -1000;
2242    } else if (oom_score_adjusted_by_redis) {
2243        oom_score_adjusted_by_redis = 0;
2244        val = oom_score_adj_base;
2245    }
2246    else {
2247        return C_OK;
2248    }
2249
2250    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf) - 1, "%d\n", val);
2251
2252    fd = open("/proc/self/oom_score_adj", O_WRONLY);
2253    if (fd < 0 || write(fd, buf, strlen(buf)) < 0) {
2254        serverLog(LL_WARNING, "Unable to write oom_score_adj: %s", strerror(errno));
2255        if (fd != -1) close(fd);
2256        return C_ERR;
2257    }
2258
2259    close(fd);
2260    return C_OK;
2261#else
2262    /* Unsupported */
2263    return C_ERR;
2264#endif
2265}
2266
2267/* This function will try to raise the max number of open files accordingly to
2268 * the configured max number of clients. It also reserves a number of file
2269 * descriptors (CONFIG_MIN_RESERVED_FDS) for extra operations of
2270 * persistence, listening sockets, log files and so forth.
2271 *
2272 * If it will not be possible to set the limit accordingly to the configured
2273 * max number of clients, the function will do the reverse setting
2274 * server.maxclients to the value that we can actually handle. */
2275void adjustOpenFilesLimit(void) {
2276    rlim_t maxfiles = server.maxclients+CONFIG_MIN_RESERVED_FDS;
2277    struct rlimit limit;
2278
2279    if (getrlimit(RLIMIT_NOFILE,&limit) == -1) {
2280        serverLog(LL_WARNING,"Unable to obtain the current NOFILE limit (%s), assuming 1024 and setting the max clients configuration accordingly.",
2281            strerror(errno));
2282        server.maxclients = 1024-CONFIG_MIN_RESERVED_FDS;
2283    } else {
2284        rlim_t oldlimit = limit.rlim_cur;
2285
2286        /* Set the max number of files if the current limit is not enough
2287         * for our needs. */
2288        if (oldlimit < maxfiles) {
2289            rlim_t bestlimit;
2290            int setrlimit_error = 0;
2291
2292            /* Try to set the file limit to match 'maxfiles' or at least
2293             * to the higher value supported less than maxfiles. */
2294            bestlimit = maxfiles;
2295            while(bestlimit > oldlimit) {
2296                rlim_t decr_step = 16;
2297
2298                limit.rlim_cur = bestlimit;
2299                limit.rlim_max = bestlimit;
2300                if (setrlimit(RLIMIT_NOFILE,&limit) != -1) break;
2301                setrlimit_error = errno;
2302
2303                /* We failed to set file limit to 'bestlimit'. Try with a
2304                 * smaller limit decrementing by a few FDs per iteration. */
2305                if (bestlimit < decr_step) {
2306                    bestlimit = oldlimit;
2307                    break;
2308                }
2309                bestlimit -= decr_step;
2310            }
2311
2312            /* Assume that the limit we get initially is still valid if
2313             * our last try was even lower. */
2314            if (bestlimit < oldlimit) bestlimit = oldlimit;
2315
2316            if (bestlimit < maxfiles) {
2317                unsigned int old_maxclients = server.maxclients;
2318                server.maxclients = bestlimit-CONFIG_MIN_RESERVED_FDS;
2319                /* maxclients is unsigned so may overflow: in order
2320                 * to check if maxclients is now logically less than 1
2321                 * we test indirectly via bestlimit. */
2322                if (bestlimit <= CONFIG_MIN_RESERVED_FDS) {
2323                    serverLog(LL_WARNING,"Your current 'ulimit -n' "
2324                        "of %llu is not enough for the server to start. "
2325                        "Please increase your open file limit to at least "
2326                        "%llu. Exiting.",
2327                        (unsigned long long) oldlimit,
2328                        (unsigned long long) maxfiles);
2329                    exit(1);
2330                }
2331                serverLog(LL_WARNING,"You requested maxclients of %d "
2332                    "requiring at least %llu max file descriptors.",
2333                    old_maxclients,
2334                    (unsigned long long) maxfiles);
2335                serverLog(LL_WARNING,"Server can't set maximum open files "
2336                    "to %llu because of OS error: %s.",
2337                    (unsigned long long) maxfiles, strerror(setrlimit_error));
2338                serverLog(LL_WARNING,"Current maximum open files is %llu. "
2339                    "maxclients has been reduced to %d to compensate for "
2340                    "low ulimit. "
2341                    "If you need higher maxclients increase 'ulimit -n'.",
2342                    (unsigned long long) bestlimit, server.maxclients);
2343            } else {
2344                serverLog(LL_NOTICE,"Increased maximum number of open files "
2345                    "to %llu (it was originally set to %llu).",
2346                    (unsigned long long) maxfiles,
2347                    (unsigned long long) oldlimit);
2348            }
2349        }
2350    }
2351}
2352
2353/* Check that server.tcp_backlog can be actually enforced in Linux according
2354 * to the value of /proc/sys/net/core/somaxconn, or warn about it. */
2355void checkTcpBacklogSettings(void) {
2356#if defined(HAVE_PROC_SOMAXCONN)
2357    FILE *fp = fopen("/proc/sys/net/core/somaxconn","r");
2358    char buf[1024];
2359    if (!fp) return;
2360    if (fgets(buf,sizeof(buf),fp) != NULL) {
2361        int somaxconn = atoi(buf);
2362        if (somaxconn > 0 && somaxconn < server.tcp_backlog) {
2363            serverLog(LL_WARNING,"WARNING: The TCP backlog setting of %d cannot be enforced because /proc/sys/net/core/somaxconn is set to the lower value of %d.", server.tcp_backlog, somaxconn);
2364        }
2365    }
2366    fclose(fp);
2367#elif defined(HAVE_SYSCTL_KIPC_SOMAXCONN)
2368    int somaxconn, mib[3];
2369    size_t len = sizeof(int);
2370
2371    mib[0] = CTL_KERN;
2372    mib[1] = KERN_IPC;
2373    mib[2] = KIPC_SOMAXCONN;
2374
2375    if (sysctl(mib, 3, &somaxconn, &len, NULL, 0) == 0) {
2376        if (somaxconn > 0 && somaxconn < server.tcp_backlog) {
2377            serverLog(LL_WARNING,"WARNING: The TCP backlog setting of %d cannot be enforced because kern.ipc.somaxconn is set to the lower value of %d.", server.tcp_backlog, somaxconn);
2378        }
2379    }
2380#elif defined(HAVE_SYSCTL_KERN_SOMAXCONN)
2381    int somaxconn, mib[2];
2382    size_t len = sizeof(int);
2383
2384    mib[0] = CTL_KERN;
2385    mib[1] = KERN_SOMAXCONN;
2386
2387    if (sysctl(mib, 2, &somaxconn, &len, NULL, 0) == 0) {
2388        if (somaxconn > 0 && somaxconn < server.tcp_backlog) {
2389            serverLog(LL_WARNING,"WARNING: The TCP backlog setting of %d cannot be enforced because kern.somaxconn is set to the lower value of %d.", server.tcp_backlog, somaxconn);
2390        }
2391    }
2392#elif defined(SOMAXCONN)
2393    if (SOMAXCONN < server.tcp_backlog) {
2394        serverLog(LL_WARNING,"WARNING: The TCP backlog setting of %d cannot be enforced because SOMAXCONN is set to the lower value of %d.", server.tcp_backlog, SOMAXCONN);
2395    }
2396#endif
2397}
2398
2399void closeListener(connListener *sfd) {
2400    int j;
2401
2402    for (j = 0; j < sfd->count; j++) {
2403        if (sfd->fd[j] == -1) continue;
2404
2405        aeDeleteFileEvent(server.el, sfd->fd[j], AE_READABLE);
2406        close(sfd->fd[j]);
2407    }
2408
2409    sfd->count = 0;
2410}
2411
2412/* Create an event handler for accepting new connections in TCP or TLS domain sockets.
2413 * This works atomically for all socket fds */
2414int createSocketAcceptHandler(connListener *sfd, aeFileProc *accept_handler) {
2415    int j;
2416
2417    for (j = 0; j < sfd->count; j++) {
2418        if (aeCreateFileEvent(server.el, sfd->fd[j], AE_READABLE, accept_handler,sfd) == AE_ERR) {
2419            /* Rollback */
2420            for (j = j-1; j >= 0; j--) aeDeleteFileEvent(server.el, sfd->fd[j], AE_READABLE);
2421            return C_ERR;
2422        }
2423    }
2424    return C_OK;
2425}
2426
2427/* Initialize a set of file descriptors to listen to the specified 'port'
2428 * binding the addresses specified in the Redis server configuration.
2429 *
2430 * The listening file descriptors are stored in the integer array 'fds'
2431 * and their number is set in '*count'. Actually @sfd should be 'listener',
2432 * for the historical reasons, let's keep 'sfd' here.
2433 *
2434 * The addresses to bind are specified in the global server.bindaddr array
2435 * and their number is server.bindaddr_count. If the server configuration
2436 * contains no specific addresses to bind, this function will try to
2437 * bind * (all addresses) for both the IPv4 and IPv6 protocols.
2438 *
2439 * On success the function returns C_OK.
2440 *
2441 * On error the function returns C_ERR. For the function to be on
2442 * error, at least one of the server.bindaddr addresses was
2443 * impossible to bind, or no bind addresses were specified in the server
2444 * configuration but the function is not able to bind * for at least
2445 * one of the IPv4 or IPv6 protocols. */
2446int listenToPort(connListener *sfd) {
2447    int j;
2448    int port = sfd->port;
2449    char **bindaddr = sfd->bindaddr;
2450
2451    /* If we have no bind address, we don't listen on a TCP socket */
2452    if (sfd->bindaddr_count == 0) return C_OK;
2453
2454    for (j = 0; j < sfd->bindaddr_count; j++) {
2455        char* addr = bindaddr[j];
2456        int optional = *addr == '-';
2457        if (optional) addr++;
2458        if (strchr(addr,':')) {
2459            /* Bind IPv6 address. */
2460            sfd->fd[sfd->count] = anetTcp6Server(server.neterr,port,addr,server.tcp_backlog);
2461        } else {
2462            /* Bind IPv4 address. */
2463            sfd->fd[sfd->count] = anetTcpServer(server.neterr,port,addr,server.tcp_backlog);
2464        }
2465        if (sfd->fd[sfd->count] == ANET_ERR) {
2466            int net_errno = errno;
2467            serverLog(LL_WARNING,
2468                "Warning: Could not create server TCP listening socket %s:%d: %s",
2469                addr, port, server.neterr);
2470            if (net_errno == EADDRNOTAVAIL && optional)
2471                continue;
2472            if (net_errno == ENOPROTOOPT     || net_errno == EPROTONOSUPPORT ||
2473                net_errno == ESOCKTNOSUPPORT || net_errno == EPFNOSUPPORT ||
2474                net_errno == EAFNOSUPPORT)
2475                continue;
2476
2477            /* Rollback successful listens before exiting */
2478            closeListener(sfd);
2479            return C_ERR;
2480        }
2481        if (server.socket_mark_id > 0) anetSetSockMarkId(NULL, sfd->fd[sfd->count], server.socket_mark_id);
2482        anetNonBlock(NULL,sfd->fd[sfd->count]);
2483        anetCloexec(sfd->fd[sfd->count]);
2484        sfd->count++;
2485    }
2486    return C_OK;
2487}

2489/* Resets the stats that we expose via INFO or other means that we want
2490 * to reset via CONFIG RESETSTAT. The function is also used in order to
2491 * initialize these fields in initServer() at server startup. */
2492void resetServerStats(void) {
2493    int j;
2494
2495    server.stat_numcommands = 0;
2496    server.stat_numconnections = 0;
2497    server.stat_expiredkeys = 0;
2498    server.stat_expired_stale_perc = 0;
2499    server.stat_expired_time_cap_reached_count = 0;
2500    server.stat_expire_cycle_time_used = 0;
2501    server.stat_evictedkeys = 0;
2502    server.stat_evictedclients = 0;
2503    server.stat_total_eviction_exceeded_time = 0;
2504    server.stat_last_eviction_exceeded_time = 0;
2505    server.stat_keyspace_misses = 0;
2506    server.stat_keyspace_hits = 0;
2507    server.stat_active_defrag_hits = 0;
2508    server.stat_active_defrag_misses = 0;
2509    server.stat_active_defrag_key_hits = 0;
2510    server.stat_active_defrag_key_misses = 0;
2511    server.stat_active_defrag_scanned = 0;
2512    server.stat_total_active_defrag_time = 0;
2513    server.stat_last_active_defrag_time = 0;
2514    server.stat_fork_time = 0;
2515    server.stat_fork_rate = 0;
2516    server.stat_total_forks = 0;
2517    server.stat_rejected_conn = 0;
2518    server.stat_sync_full = 0;
2519    server.stat_sync_partial_ok = 0;
2520    server.stat_sync_partial_err = 0;
2521    server.stat_io_reads_processed = 0;
2522    atomicSet(server.stat_total_reads_processed, 0);
2523    server.stat_io_writes_processed = 0;
2524    atomicSet(server.stat_total_writes_processed, 0);
2525    for (j = 0; j < STATS_METRIC_COUNT; j++) {
2526        server.inst_metric[j].idx = 0;
2527        server.inst_metric[j].last_sample_base = 0;
2528        server.inst_metric[j].last_sample_value = 0;

2530            sizeof(server.inst_metric[j].samples));
2531    }
2532    server.stat_aof_rewrites = 0;
2533    server.stat_rdb_saves = 0;

2536    atomicSet(server.stat_net_output_bytes, 0);
2537    atomicSet(server.stat_net_repl_input_bytes, 0);
2538    atomicSet(server.stat_net_repl_output_bytes, 0);
2539    server.stat_unexpected_error_replies = 0;
2540    server.stat_total_error_replies = 0;
2541    server.stat_dump_payload_sanitizations = 0;
2542    server.aof_delayed_fsync = 0;
2543    server.stat_reply_buffer_shrinks = 0;
2544    server.stat_reply_buffer_expands = 0;
2545    memset(server.duration_stats, 0, sizeof(durationStats) * EL_DURATION_TYPE_NUM);
2546    server.el_cmd_cnt_max = 0;
2547    lazyfreeResetStats();
2548}
2549
2550/* Make the thread killable at any time, so that kill threads functions
2551 * can work reliably (default cancelability type is PTHREAD_CANCEL_DEFERRED).
2552 * Needed for pthread_cancel used by the fast memory test used by the crash report. */
2553void makeThreadKillable(void) {
2554    pthread_setcancelstate(PTHREAD_CANCEL_ENABLE, NULL);
2555    pthread_setcanceltype(PTHREAD_CANCEL_ASYNCHRONOUS, NULL);
2556}
2557
2558void initServer(void) {
2559    int j;
2560
2561    signal(SIGHUP, SIG_IGN);
2562    signal(SIGPIPE, SIG_IGN);
2563    setupSignalHandlers();
2564    makeThreadKillable();
2565
2566    if (server.syslog_enabled) {
2567        openlog(server.syslog_ident, LOG_PID | LOG_NDELAY | LOG_NOWAIT,
2568            server.syslog_facility);
2569    }
2570
2571    /* Initialization after setting defaults from the config system. */
2572    server.aof_state = server.aof_enabled ? AOF_ON : AOF_OFF;
2573    server.fsynced_reploff = server.aof_enabled ? 0 : -1;
2574    server.hz = server.config_hz;
2575    server.pid = getpid();
2576    server.in_fork_child = CHILD_TYPE_NONE;
2577    server.main_thread_id = pthread_self();
2578    server.current_client = NULL;
2579    server.errors = raxNew();
2580    server.execution_nesting = 0;
2581    server.clients = listCreate();
2582    server.clients_index = raxNew();
2583    server.clients_to_close = listCreate();
2584    server.slaves = listCreate();
2585    server.monitors = listCreate();
2586    server.clients_pending_write = listCreate();
2587    server.clients_pending_read = listCreate();
2588    server.clients_timeout_table = raxNew();
2589    server.replication_allowed = 1;
2590    server.slaveseldb = -1; /* Force to emit the first SELECT command. */
2591    server.unblocked_clients = listCreate();
2592    server.ready_keys = listCreate();
2593    server.tracking_pending_keys = listCreate();
2594    server.pending_push_messages = listCreate();
2595    server.clients_waiting_acks = listCreate();
2596    server.get_ack_from_slaves = 0;
2597    server.paused_actions = 0;
2598    memset(server.client_pause_per_purpose, 0,
2599           sizeof(server.client_pause_per_purpose));
2600    server.postponed_clients = listCreate();
2601    server.events_processed_while_blocked = 0;
2602    server.system_memory_size = zmalloc_get_memory_size();
2603    server.blocked_last_cron = 0;
2604    server.blocking_op_nesting = 0;
2605    server.thp_enabled = 0;
2606    server.cluster_drop_packet_filter = -1;
2607    server.reply_buffer_peak_reset_time = REPLY_BUFFER_DEFAULT_PEAK_RESET_TIME;
2608    server.reply_buffer_resizing_enabled = 1;
2609    server.client_mem_usage_buckets = NULL;
2610    resetReplicationBuffer();
2611
2612    /* Make sure the locale is set on startup based on the config file. */
2613    if (setlocale(LC_COLLATE,server.locale_collate) == NULL) {
2614        serverLog(LL_WARNING, "Failed to configure LOCALE for invalid locale name.");
2615        exit(1);
2616    }
2617
2618    createSharedObjects();
2619    adjustOpenFilesLimit();
2620    const char *clk_msg = monotonicInit();
2621    serverLog(LL_NOTICE, "monotonic clock: %s", clk_msg);
2622    server.el = aeCreateEventLoop(server.maxclients+CONFIG_FDSET_INCR);
2623    if (server.el == NULL) {
2624        serverLog(LL_WARNING,
2625            "Failed creating the event loop. Error message: '%s'",
2626            strerror(errno));
2627        exit(1);
2628    }
2629    server.db = zmalloc(sizeof(redisDb)*server.dbnum);
2630
2631    /* Create the Redis databases, and initialize other internal state. */
2632    for (j = 0; j < server.dbnum; j++) {
2633        server.db[j].dict = dictCreate(&dbDictType);
2634        server.db[j].expires = dictCreate(&dbExpiresDictType);
2635        server.db[j].expires_cursor = 0;
2636        server.db[j].blocking_keys = dictCreate(&keylistDictType);
2637        server.db[j].blocking_keys_unblock_on_nokey = dictCreate(&objectKeyPointerValueDictType);
2638        server.db[j].ready_keys = dictCreate(&objectKeyPointerValueDictType);
2639        server.db[j].watched_keys = dictCreate(&keylistDictType);
2640        server.db[j].id = j;
2641        server.db[j].avg_ttl = 0;
2642        server.db[j].defrag_later = listCreate();
2643        server.db[j].slots_to_keys = NULL; /* Set by clusterInit later on if necessary. */
2644        listSetFreeMethod(server.db[j].defrag_later,(void (*)(void*))sdsfree);
2645    }
2646    evictionPoolAlloc(); /* Initialize the LRU keys pool. */
2647    server.pubsub_channels = dictCreate(&keylistDictType);
2648    server.pubsub_patterns = dictCreate(&keylistDictType);
2649    server.pubsubshard_channels = dictCreate(&keylistDictType);
2650    server.cronloops = 0;
2651    server.in_exec = 0;
2652    server.busy_module_yield_flags = BUSY_MODULE_YIELD_NONE;
2653    server.busy_module_yield_reply = NULL;
2654    server.client_pause_in_transaction = 0;
2655    server.child_pid = -1;
2656    server.child_type = CHILD_TYPE_NONE;
2657    server.rdb_child_type = RDB_CHILD_TYPE_NONE;
2658    server.rdb_pipe_conns = NULL;
2659    server.rdb_pipe_numconns = 0;
2660    server.rdb_pipe_numconns_writing = 0;
2661    server.rdb_pipe_buff = NULL;
2662    server.rdb_pipe_bufflen = 0;
2663    server.rdb_bgsave_scheduled = 0;
2664    server.child_info_pipe[0] = -1;
2665    server.child_info_pipe[1] = -1;
2666    server.child_info_nread = 0;
2667    server.aof_buf = sdsempty();
2668    server.lastsave = time(NULL); /* At startup we consider the DB saved. */
2669    server.lastbgsave_try = 0;    /* At startup we never tried to BGSAVE. */
2670    server.rdb_save_time_last = -1;
2671    server.rdb_save_time_start = -1;
2672    server.rdb_last_load_keys_expired = 0;
2673    server.rdb_last_load_keys_loaded = 0;
2674    server.dirty = 0;
2675    resetServerStats();
2676    /* A few stats we don't want to reset: server startup time, and peak mem. */
2677    server.stat_starttime = time(NULL);
2678    server.stat_peak_memory = 0;
2679    server.stat_current_cow_peak = 0;
2680    server.stat_current_cow_bytes = 0;
2681    server.stat_current_cow_updated = 0;
2682    server.stat_current_save_keys_processed = 0;
2683    server.stat_current_save_keys_total = 0;
2684    server.stat_rdb_cow_bytes = 0;
2685    server.stat_aof_cow_bytes = 0;
2686    server.stat_module_cow_bytes = 0;
2687    server.stat_module_progress = 0;
2688    for (int j = 0; j < CLIENT_TYPE_COUNT; j++)
2689        server.stat_clients_type_memory[j] = 0;
2690    server.stat_cluster_links_memory = 0;
2691    server.cron_malloc_stats.zmalloc_used = 0;
2692    server.cron_malloc_stats.process_rss = 0;
2693    server.cron_malloc_stats.allocator_allocated = 0;
2694    server.cron_malloc_stats.allocator_active = 0;
2695    server.cron_malloc_stats.allocator_resident = 0;
2696    server.lastbgsave_status = C_OK;
2697    server.aof_last_write_status = C_OK;
2698    server.aof_last_write_errno = 0;
2699    server.repl_good_slaves_count = 0;
2700    server.last_sig_received = 0;
2701
2702    /* Initiate acl info struct */
2703    server.acl_info.invalid_cmd_accesses = 0;
2704    server.acl_info.invalid_key_accesses  = 0;
2705    server.acl_info.user_auth_failures = 0;
2706    server.acl_info.invalid_channel_accesses = 0;
2707
2708    /* Create the timer callback, this is our way to process many background
2709     * operations incrementally, like clients timeout, eviction of unaccessed
2710     * expired keys and so forth. */
2711    if (aeCreateTimeEvent(server.el, 1, serverCron, NULL, NULL) == AE_ERR) {
2712        serverPanic("Can't create event loop timers.");
2713        exit(1);
2714    }
2715
2716    /* Register a readable event for the pipe used to awake the event loop
2717     * from module threads. */
2718    if (aeCreateFileEvent(server.el, server.module_pipe[0], AE_READABLE,
2719        modulePipeReadable,NULL) == AE_ERR) {
2720            serverPanic(
2721                "Error registering the readable event for the module pipe.");
2722    }
2723
2724    /* Register before and after sleep handlers (note this needs to be done
2725     * before loading persistence since it is used by processEventsWhileBlocked. */
2726    aeSetBeforeSleepProc(server.el,beforeSleep);
2727    aeSetAfterSleepProc(server.el,afterSleep);
2728
2729    /* 32 bit instances are limited to 4GB of address space, so if there is
2730     * no explicit limit in the user provided configuration we set a limit
2731     * at 3 GB using maxmemory with 'noeviction' policy'. This avoids
2732     * useless crashes of the Redis instance for out of memory. */
2733    if (server.arch_bits == 32 && server.maxmemory == 0) {
2734        serverLog(LL_WARNING,"Warning: 32 bit instance detected but no memory limit set. Setting 3 GB maxmemory limit with 'noeviction' policy now.");
2735        server.maxmemory = 3072LL*(1024*1024); /* 3 GB */
2736        server.maxmemory_policy = MAXMEMORY_NO_EVICTION;
2737    }
2738
2739    scriptingInit(1);
2740    functionsInit();
2741    slowlogInit();
2742    latencyMonitorInit();
2743
2744    /* Initialize ACL default password if it exists */
2745    ACLUpdateDefaultUserPassword(server.requirepass);
2746
2747    applyWatchdogPeriod();
2748
2749    if (server.maxmemory_clients != 0)
2750        initServerClientMemUsageBuckets();
2751}
2752
2753void initListeners(void) {
2754    /* Setup listeners from server config for TCP/TLS/Unix */
2755    int conn_index;
2756    connListener *listener;
2757    if (server.port != 0) {
2758        conn_index = connectionIndexByType(CONN_TYPE_SOCKET);
2759        if (conn_index < 0)
2760            serverPanic("Failed finding connection listener of %s", CONN_TYPE_SOCKET);
2761        listener = &server.listeners[conn_index];
2762        listener->bindaddr = server.bindaddr;
2763        listener->bindaddr_count = server.bindaddr_count;
2764        listener->port = server.port;
2765        listener->ct = connectionByType(CONN_TYPE_SOCKET);
2766    }
2767
2768    if (server.tls_port || server.tls_replication || server.tls_cluster) {
2769        ConnectionType *ct_tls = connectionTypeTls();
2770        if (!ct_tls) {
2771            serverLog(LL_WARNING, "Failed finding TLS support.");
2772            exit(1);
2773        }
2774        if (connTypeConfigure(ct_tls, &server.tls_ctx_config, 1) == C_ERR) {
2775            serverLog(LL_WARNING, "Failed to configure TLS. Check logs for more info.");
2776            exit(1);
2777        }
2778    }
2779
2780    if (server.tls_port != 0) {
2781        conn_index = connectionIndexByType(CONN_TYPE_TLS);
2782        if (conn_index < 0)
2783            serverPanic("Failed finding connection listener of %s", CONN_TYPE_TLS);
2784        listener = &server.listeners[conn_index];
2785        listener->bindaddr = server.bindaddr;
2786        listener->bindaddr_count = server.bindaddr_count;
2787        listener->port = server.tls_port;
2788        listener->ct = connectionByType(CONN_TYPE_TLS);
2789    }
2790    if (server.unixsocket != NULL) {
2791        conn_index = connectionIndexByType(CONN_TYPE_UNIX);
2792        if (conn_index < 0)
2793            serverPanic("Failed finding connection listener of %s", CONN_TYPE_UNIX);
2794        listener = &server.listeners[conn_index];
2795        listener->bindaddr = &server.unixsocket;
2796        listener->bindaddr_count = 1;
2797        listener->ct = connectionByType(CONN_TYPE_UNIX);
2798        listener->priv = &server.unixsocketperm; /* Unix socket specified */
2799    }
2800
2801    /* create all the configured listener, and add handler to start to accept */
2802    int listen_fds = 0;
2803    for (int j = 0; j < CONN_TYPE_MAX; j++) {
2804        listener = &server.listeners[j];
2805        if (listener->ct == NULL)
2806            continue;
2807
2808        if (connListen(listener) == C_ERR) {
2809            serverLog(LL_WARNING, "Failed listening on port %u (%s), aborting.", listener->port, listener->ct->get_type(NULL));
2810            exit(1);
2811        }
2812
2813        if (createSocketAcceptHandler(listener, connAcceptHandler(listener->ct)) != C_OK)
2814            serverPanic("Unrecoverable error creating %s listener accept handler.", listener->ct->get_type(NULL));
2815
2816       listen_fds += listener->count;
2817    }
2818
2819    if (listen_fds == 0) {
2820        serverLog(LL_WARNING, "Configured to not listen anywhere, exiting.");
2821        exit(1);
2822    }
2823}
2824
2825/* Some steps in server initialization need to be done last (after modules
2826 * are loaded).
2827 * Specifically, creation of threads due to a race bug in ld.so, in which
2828 * Thread Local Storage initialization collides with dlopen call.
2829 * see: https://sourceware.org/bugzilla/show_bug.cgi?id=19329 */
2830void InitServerLast(void) {
2831    bioInit();
2832    initThreadedIO();
2833    set_jemalloc_bg_thread(server.jemalloc_bg_thread);
2834    server.initial_memory_usage = zmalloc_used_memory();
2835}
2836
2837/* The purpose of this function is to try to "glue" consecutive range
2838 * key specs in order to build the legacy (first,last,step) spec
2839 * used by the COMMAND command.
2840 * By far the most common case is just one range spec (e.g. SET)
2841 * but some commands' ranges were split into two or more ranges
2842 * in order to have different flags for different keys (e.g. SMOVE,
2843 * first key is "RW ACCESS DELETE", second key is "RW INSERT").
2844 *
2845 * Additionally set the CMD_MOVABLE_KEYS flag for commands that may have key
2846 * names in their arguments, but the legacy range spec doesn't cover all of them.
2847 *
2848 * This function uses very basic heuristics and is "best effort":
2849 * 1. Only commands which have only "range" specs are considered.
2850 * 2. Only range specs with keystep of 1 are considered.
2851 * 3. The order of the range specs must be ascending (i.e.
2852 *    lastkey of spec[i] == firstkey-1 of spec[i+1]).
2853 *
2854 * This function will succeed on all native Redis commands and may
2855 * fail on module commands, even if it only has "range" specs that
2856 * could actually be "glued", in the following cases:
2857 * 1. The order of "range" specs is not ascending (e.g. the spec for
2858 *    the key at index 2 was added before the spec of the key at
2859 *    index 1).
2860 * 2. The "range" specs have keystep >1.
2861 *
2862 * If this functions fails it means that the legacy (first,last,step)
2863 * spec used by COMMAND will show 0,0,0. This is not a dire situation
2864 * because anyway the legacy (first,last,step) spec is to be deprecated
2865 * and one should use the new key specs scheme.
2866 */
2867void populateCommandLegacyRangeSpec(struct redisCommand *c) {
2868    memset(&c->legacy_range_key_spec, 0, sizeof(c->legacy_range_key_spec));
2869
2870    /* Set the movablekeys flag if we have a GETKEYS flag for modules.
2871     * Note that for native redis commands, we always have keyspecs,
2872     * with enough information to rely on for movablekeys. */
2873    if (c->flags & CMD_MODULE_GETKEYS)
2874        c->flags |= CMD_MOVABLE_KEYS;
2875
2876    /* no key-specs, no keys, exit. */
2877    if (c->key_specs_num == 0) {
2878        return;
2879    }
2880
2881    if (c->key_specs_num == 1 &&
2882        c->key_specs[0].begin_search_type == KSPEC_BS_INDEX &&
2883        c->key_specs[0].find_keys_type == KSPEC_FK_RANGE)
2884    {
2885        /* Quick win, exactly one range spec. */
2886        c->legacy_range_key_spec = c->key_specs[0];
2887        /* If it has the incomplete flag, set the movablekeys flag on the command. */
2888        if (c->key_specs[0].flags & CMD_KEY_INCOMPLETE)
2889            c->flags |= CMD_MOVABLE_KEYS;
2890        return;
2891    }
2892
2893    int firstkey = INT_MAX, lastkey = 0;
2894    int prev_lastkey = 0;
2895    for (int i = 0; i < c->key_specs_num; i++) {
2896        if (c->key_specs[i].begin_search_type != KSPEC_BS_INDEX ||
2897            c->key_specs[i].find_keys_type != KSPEC_FK_RANGE)
2898        {
2899            /* Found an incompatible (non range) spec, skip it, and set the movablekeys flag. */
2900            c->flags |= CMD_MOVABLE_KEYS;
2901            continue;
2902        }
2903        if (c->key_specs[i].fk.range.keystep != 1 ||
2904            (prev_lastkey && prev_lastkey != c->key_specs[i].bs.index.pos-1))
2905        {
2906            /* Found a range spec that's not plain (step of 1) or not consecutive to the previous one.
2907             * Skip it, and we set the movablekeys flag. */
2908            c->flags |= CMD_MOVABLE_KEYS;
2909            continue;
2910        }
2911        if (c->key_specs[i].flags & CMD_KEY_INCOMPLETE) {
2912            /* The spec we're using is incomplete, we can use it, but we also have to set the movablekeys flag. */
2913            c->flags |= CMD_MOVABLE_KEYS;
2914        }
2915        firstkey = min(firstkey, c->key_specs[i].bs.index.pos);
2916        /* Get the absolute index for lastkey (in the "range" spec, lastkey is relative to firstkey) */
2917        int lastkey_abs_index = c->key_specs[i].fk.range.lastkey;
2918        if (lastkey_abs_index >= 0)
2919            lastkey_abs_index += c->key_specs[i].bs.index.pos;
2920        /* For lastkey we use unsigned comparison to handle negative values correctly */
2921        lastkey = max((unsigned)lastkey, (unsigned)lastkey_abs_index);
2922        prev_lastkey = lastkey;
2923    }
2924
2925    if (firstkey == INT_MAX) {
2926        /* Couldn't find range specs, the legacy range spec will remain empty, and we set the movablekeys flag. */
2927        c->flags |= CMD_MOVABLE_KEYS;
2928        return;
2929    }
2930
2931    serverAssert(firstkey != 0);
2932    serverAssert(lastkey != 0);
2933
2934    c->legacy_range_key_spec.begin_search_type = KSPEC_BS_INDEX;
2935    c->legacy_range_key_spec.bs.index.pos = firstkey;
2936    c->legacy_range_key_spec.find_keys_type = KSPEC_FK_RANGE;
2937    c->legacy_range_key_spec.fk.range.lastkey = lastkey < 0 ? lastkey : (lastkey-firstkey); /* in the "range" spec, lastkey is relative to firstkey */
2938    c->legacy_range_key_spec.fk.range.keystep = 1;
2939    c->legacy_range_key_spec.fk.range.limit = 0;
2940}
2941
2942sds catSubCommandFullname(const char *parent_name, const char *sub_name) {
2943    return sdscatfmt(sdsempty(), "%s|%s", parent_name, sub_name);
2944}
2945
2946void commandAddSubcommand(struct redisCommand *parent, struct redisCommand *subcommand, const char *declared_name) {
2947    if (!parent->subcommands_dict)
2948        parent->subcommands_dict = dictCreate(&commandTableDictType);
2949
2950    subcommand->parent = parent; /* Assign the parent command */
2951    subcommand->id = ACLGetCommandID(subcommand->fullname); /* Assign the ID used for ACL. */
2952
2953    serverAssert(dictAdd(parent->subcommands_dict, sdsnew(declared_name), subcommand) == DICT_OK);
2954}
2955
2956/* Set implicit ACl categories (see comment above the definition of
2957 * struct redisCommand). */
2958void setImplicitACLCategories(struct redisCommand *c) {
2959    if (c->flags & CMD_WRITE)
2960        c->acl_categories |= ACL_CATEGORY_WRITE;
2961    /* Exclude scripting commands from the RO category. */
2962    if (c->flags & CMD_READONLY && !(c->acl_categories & ACL_CATEGORY_SCRIPTING))
2963        c->acl_categories |= ACL_CATEGORY_READ;
2964    if (c->flags & CMD_ADMIN)
2965        c->acl_categories |= ACL_CATEGORY_ADMIN|ACL_CATEGORY_DANGEROUS;
2966    if (c->flags & CMD_PUBSUB)
2967        c->acl_categories |= ACL_CATEGORY_PUBSUB;
2968    if (c->flags & CMD_FAST)
2969        c->acl_categories |= ACL_CATEGORY_FAST;
2970    if (c->flags & CMD_BLOCKING)
2971        c->acl_categories |= ACL_CATEGORY_BLOCKING;
2972
2973    /* If it's not @fast is @slow in this binary world. */
2974    if (!(c->acl_categories & ACL_CATEGORY_FAST))
2975        c->acl_categories |= ACL_CATEGORY_SLOW;
2976}
2977
2978/* Recursively populate the command structure.
2979 *
2980 * On success, the function return C_OK. Otherwise C_ERR is returned and we won't
2981 * add this command in the commands dict. */
2982int populateCommandStructure(struct redisCommand *c) {
2983    /* If the command marks with CMD_SENTINEL, it exists in sentinel. */
2984    if (!(c->flags & CMD_SENTINEL) && server.sentinel_mode)
2985        return C_ERR;
2986
2987    /* If the command marks with CMD_ONLY_SENTINEL, it only exists in sentinel. */
2988    if (c->flags & CMD_ONLY_SENTINEL && !server.sentinel_mode)
2989        return C_ERR;
2990
2991    /* Translate the command string flags description into an actual
2992     * set of flags. */
2993    setImplicitACLCategories(c);
2994
2995    /* We start with an unallocated histogram and only allocate memory when a command
2996     * has been issued for the first time */
2997    c->latency_histogram = NULL;
2998
2999    /* Handle the legacy range spec and the "movablekeys" flag (must be done after populating all key specs). */
3000    populateCommandLegacyRangeSpec(c);
3001
3002    /* Assign the ID used for ACL. */
3003    c->id = ACLGetCommandID(c->fullname);
3004
3005    /* Handle subcommands */
3006    if (c->subcommands) {
3007        for (int j = 0; c->subcommands[j].declared_name; j++) {
3008            struct redisCommand *sub = c->subcommands+j;
3009
3010            sub->fullname = catSubCommandFullname(c->declared_name, sub->declared_name);
3011            if (populateCommandStructure(sub) == C_ERR)
3012                continue;
3013
3014            commandAddSubcommand(c, sub, sub->declared_name);
3015        }
3016    }
3017
3018    return C_OK;
3019}
3020
3021extern struct redisCommand redisCommandTable[];
3022
3023/* Populates the Redis Command Table dict from the static table in commands.c
3024 * which is auto generated from the json files in the commands folder. */
3025void populateCommandTable(void) {
3026    int j;
3027    struct redisCommand *c;
3028
3029    for (j = 0;; j++) {
3030        c = redisCommandTable + j;
3031        if (c->declared_name == NULL)
3032            break;
3033
3034        int retval1, retval2;
3035
3036        c->fullname = sdsnew(c->declared_name);
3037        if (populateCommandStructure(c) == C_ERR)
3038            continue;
3039
3040        retval1 = dictAdd(server.commands, sdsdup(c->fullname), c);
3041        /* Populate an additional dictionary that will be unaffected
3042         * by rename-command statements in redis.conf. */
3043        retval2 = dictAdd(server.orig_commands, sdsdup(c->fullname), c);
3044        serverAssert(retval1 == DICT_OK && retval2 == DICT_OK);
3045    }
3046}
3047
3048void resetCommandTableStats(dict* commands) {
3049    struct redisCommand *c;
3050    dictEntry *de;
3051    dictIterator *di;
3052
3053    di = dictGetSafeIterator(commands);
3054    while((de = dictNext(di)) != NULL) {
3055        c = (struct redisCommand *) dictGetVal(de);
3056        c->microseconds = 0;
3057        c->calls = 0;
3058        c->rejected_calls = 0;
3059        c->failed_calls = 0;
3060        if(c->latency_histogram) {
3061            hdr_close(c->latency_histogram);
3062            c->latency_histogram = NULL;
3063        }
3064        if (c->subcommands_dict)
3065            resetCommandTableStats(c->subcommands_dict);
3066    }
3067    dictReleaseIterator(di);
3068}
3069
3070void resetErrorTableStats(void) {
3071    raxFreeWithCallback(server.errors, zfree);
3072    server.errors = raxNew();
3073}
3074
3075/* ========================== Redis OP Array API ============================ */
3076
3077int redisOpArrayAppend(redisOpArray *oa, int dbid, robj **argv, int argc, int target) {
3078    redisOp *op;
3079    int prev_capacity = oa->capacity;
3080
3081    if (oa->numops == 0) {
3082        oa->capacity = 16;
3083    } else if (oa->numops >= oa->capacity) {
3084        oa->capacity *= 2;
3085    }
3086
3087    if (prev_capacity != oa->capacity)
3088        oa->ops = zrealloc(oa->ops,sizeof(redisOp)*oa->capacity);
3089    op = oa->ops+oa->numops;
3090    op->dbid = dbid;
3091    op->argv = argv;
3092    op->argc = argc;
3093    op->target = target;
3094    oa->numops++;
3095    return oa->numops;
3096}
3097
3098void redisOpArrayFree(redisOpArray *oa) {
3099    while(oa->numops) {
3100        int j;
3101        redisOp *op;
3102
3103        oa->numops--;
3104        op = oa->ops+oa->numops;
3105        for (j = 0; j < op->argc; j++)
3106            decrRefCount(op->argv[j]);
3107        zfree(op->argv);
3108    }
3109    /* no need to free the actual op array, we reuse the memory for future commands */
3110    serverAssert(!oa->numops);
3111}
