4760void addReplyCommandTips(client *c, struct redisCommand *cmd) {
4761    addReplySetLen(c, cmd->num_tips);
4762    for (int j = 0; j<cmd->num_tips; j++) {
4763        addReplyBulkCString(c, cmd->tips[j]);
4764    }
4765}
4766
4767void addReplyCommandKeySpecs(client *c, struct redisCommand *cmd) {
4768    addReplySetLen(c, cmd->key_specs_num);
4769    for (int i = 0; i < cmd->key_specs_num; i++) {
4770        int maplen = 3;
4771        if (cmd->key_specs[i].notes) maplen++;
4772
4773        addReplyMapLen(c, maplen);
4774
4775        if (cmd->key_specs[i].notes) {
4776            addReplyBulkCString(c, "notes");
4777            addReplyBulkCString(c,cmd->key_specs[i].notes);
4778        }
4779
4780        addReplyBulkCString(c, "flags");
4781        addReplyFlagsForKeyArgs(c,cmd->key_specs[i].flags);
4782
4783        addReplyBulkCString(c, "begin_search");
4784        switch (cmd->key_specs[i].begin_search_type) {
4785            case KSPEC_BS_UNKNOWN:
4786                addReplyMapLen(c, 2);
4787                addReplyBulkCString(c, "type");
4788                addReplyBulkCString(c, "unknown");
4789
4790                addReplyBulkCString(c, "spec");
4791                addReplyMapLen(c, 0);
4792                break;
4793            case KSPEC_BS_INDEX:
4794                addReplyMapLen(c, 2);
4795                addReplyBulkCString(c, "type");
4796                addReplyBulkCString(c, "index");
4797
4798                addReplyBulkCString(c, "spec");
4799                addReplyMapLen(c, 1);
4800                addReplyBulkCString(c, "index");
4801                addReplyLongLong(c, cmd->key_specs[i].bs.index.pos);
4802                break;
4803            case KSPEC_BS_KEYWORD:
4804                addReplyMapLen(c, 2);
4805                addReplyBulkCString(c, "type");
4806                addReplyBulkCString(c, "keyword");
4807
4808                addReplyBulkCString(c, "spec");
4809                addReplyMapLen(c, 2);
4810                addReplyBulkCString(c, "keyword");
4811                addReplyBulkCString(c, cmd->key_specs[i].bs.keyword.keyword);
4812                addReplyBulkCString(c, "startfrom");
4813                addReplyLongLong(c, cmd->key_specs[i].bs.keyword.startfrom);
4814                break;
4815            default:
4816                serverPanic("Invalid begin_search key spec type %d", cmd->key_specs[i].begin_search_type);
4817        }
4818
4819        addReplyBulkCString(c, "find_keys");
4820        switch (cmd->key_specs[i].find_keys_type) {
4821            case KSPEC_FK_UNKNOWN:
4822                addReplyMapLen(c, 2);
4823                addReplyBulkCString(c, "type");
4824                addReplyBulkCString(c, "unknown");
4825
4826                addReplyBulkCString(c, "spec");
4827                addReplyMapLen(c, 0);
4828                break;
4829            case KSPEC_FK_RANGE:
4830                addReplyMapLen(c, 2);
4831                addReplyBulkCString(c, "type");
4832                addReplyBulkCString(c, "range");
4833
4834                addReplyBulkCString(c, "spec");
4835                addReplyMapLen(c, 3);
4836                addReplyBulkCString(c, "lastkey");
4837                addReplyLongLong(c, cmd->key_specs[i].fk.range.lastkey);
4838                addReplyBulkCString(c, "keystep");
4839                addReplyLongLong(c, cmd->key_specs[i].fk.range.keystep);
4840                addReplyBulkCString(c, "limit");
4841                addReplyLongLong(c, cmd->key_specs[i].fk.range.limit);
4842                break;
4843            case KSPEC_FK_KEYNUM:
4844                addReplyMapLen(c, 2);
4845                addReplyBulkCString(c, "type");
4846                addReplyBulkCString(c, "keynum");
4847
4848                addReplyBulkCString(c, "spec");
4849                addReplyMapLen(c, 3);
4850                addReplyBulkCString(c, "keynumidx");
4851                addReplyLongLong(c, cmd->key_specs[i].fk.keynum.keynumidx);
4852                addReplyBulkCString(c, "firstkey");
4853                addReplyLongLong(c, cmd->key_specs[i].fk.keynum.firstkey);
4854                addReplyBulkCString(c, "keystep");
4855                addReplyLongLong(c, cmd->key_specs[i].fk.keynum.keystep);
4856                break;
4857            default:
4858                serverPanic("Invalid find_keys key spec type %d", cmd->key_specs[i].begin_search_type);
4859        }
4860    }
4861}
4862
4863/* Reply with an array of sub-command using the provided reply callback. */
4864void addReplyCommandSubCommands(client *c, struct redisCommand *cmd, void (*reply_function)(client*, struct redisCommand*), int use_map) {
4865    if (!cmd->subcommands_dict) {
4866        addReplySetLen(c, 0);
4867        return;
4868    }
4869
4870    if (use_map)
4871        addReplyMapLen(c, dictSize(cmd->subcommands_dict));
4872    else
4873        addReplyArrayLen(c, dictSize(cmd->subcommands_dict));
4874    dictEntry *de;
4875    dictIterator *di = dictGetSafeIterator(cmd->subcommands_dict);
4876    while((de = dictNext(di)) != NULL) {
4877        struct redisCommand *sub = (struct redisCommand *)dictGetVal(de);
4878        if (use_map)
4879            addReplyBulkCBuffer(c, sub->fullname, sdslen(sub->fullname));
4880        reply_function(c, sub);
4881    }
4882    dictReleaseIterator(di);
4883}
4884
4885/* Output the representation of a Redis command. Used by the COMMAND command and COMMAND INFO. */
4886void addReplyCommandInfo(client *c, struct redisCommand *cmd) {
4887    if (!cmd) {
4888        addReplyNull(c);
4889    } else {
4890        int firstkey = 0, lastkey = 0, keystep = 0;
4891        if (cmd->legacy_range_key_spec.begin_search_type != KSPEC_BS_INVALID) {
4892            firstkey = cmd->legacy_range_key_spec.bs.index.pos;
4893            lastkey = cmd->legacy_range_key_spec.fk.range.lastkey;
4894            if (lastkey >= 0)
4895                lastkey += firstkey;
4896            keystep = cmd->legacy_range_key_spec.fk.range.keystep;
4897        }
4898
4899        addReplyArrayLen(c, 10);
4900        addReplyBulkCBuffer(c, cmd->fullname, sdslen(cmd->fullname));
4901        addReplyLongLong(c, cmd->arity);
4902        addReplyFlagsForCommand(c, cmd);
4903        addReplyLongLong(c, firstkey);
4904        addReplyLongLong(c, lastkey);
4905        addReplyLongLong(c, keystep);
4906        addReplyCommandCategories(c, cmd);
4907        addReplyCommandTips(c, cmd);
4908        addReplyCommandKeySpecs(c, cmd);
4909        addReplyCommandSubCommands(c, cmd, addReplyCommandInfo, 0);
4910    }
4911}
4912
4913/* Output the representation of a Redis command. Used by the COMMAND DOCS. */
4914void addReplyCommandDocs(client *c, struct redisCommand *cmd) {
4915    /* Count our reply len so we don't have to use deferred reply. */
4916    long maplen = 1;
4917    if (cmd->summary) maplen++;
4918    if (cmd->since) maplen++;
4919    if (cmd->flags & CMD_MODULE) maplen++;
4920    if (cmd->complexity) maplen++;
4921    if (cmd->doc_flags) maplen++;
4922    if (cmd->deprecated_since) maplen++;
4923    if (cmd->replaced_by) maplen++;
4924    if (cmd->history) maplen++;
4925#ifdef LOG_REQ_RES
4926    if (cmd->reply_schema) maplen++;
4927#endif
4928    if (cmd->args) maplen++;
4929    if (cmd->subcommands_dict) maplen++;
4930    addReplyMapLen(c, maplen);
4931
4932    if (cmd->summary) {
4933        addReplyBulkCString(c, "summary");
4934        addReplyBulkCString(c, cmd->summary);
4935    }
4936    if (cmd->since) {
4937        addReplyBulkCString(c, "since");
4938        addReplyBulkCString(c, cmd->since);
4939    }
4940
4941    /* Always have the group, for module commands the group is always "module". */
4942    addReplyBulkCString(c, "group");
4943    addReplyBulkCString(c, commandGroupStr(cmd->group));
4944
4945    if (cmd->complexity) {
4946        addReplyBulkCString(c, "complexity");
4947        addReplyBulkCString(c, cmd->complexity);
4948    }
4949    if (cmd->flags & CMD_MODULE) {
4950        addReplyBulkCString(c, "module");
4951        addReplyBulkCString(c, moduleNameFromCommand(cmd));
4952    }
4953    if (cmd->doc_flags) {
4954        addReplyBulkCString(c, "doc_flags");
4955        addReplyDocFlagsForCommand(c, cmd);
4956    }
4957    if (cmd->deprecated_since) {
4958        addReplyBulkCString(c, "deprecated_since");
4959        addReplyBulkCString(c, cmd->deprecated_since);
4960    }
4961    if (cmd->replaced_by) {
4962        addReplyBulkCString(c, "replaced_by");
4963        addReplyBulkCString(c, cmd->replaced_by);
4964    }
4965    if (cmd->history) {
4966        addReplyBulkCString(c, "history");
4967        addReplyCommandHistory(c, cmd);
4968    }
4969#ifdef LOG_REQ_RES
4970    if (cmd->reply_schema) {
4971        addReplyBulkCString(c, "reply_schema");
4972        addReplyJson(c, cmd->reply_schema);
4973    }
4974#endif
4975    if (cmd->args) {
4976        addReplyBulkCString(c, "arguments");
4977        addReplyCommandArgList(c, cmd->args, cmd->num_args);
4978    }
4979    if (cmd->subcommands_dict) {
4980        addReplyBulkCString(c, "subcommands");
4981        addReplyCommandSubCommands(c, cmd, addReplyCommandDocs, 1);
4982    }
4983}
4984
4985/* Helper for COMMAND GETKEYS and GETKEYSANDFLAGS */
4986void getKeysSubcommandImpl(client *c, int with_flags) {
4987    struct redisCommand *cmd = lookupCommand(c->argv+2,c->argc-2);
4988    getKeysResult result = GETKEYS_RESULT_INIT;
4989    int j;
4990
4991    if (!cmd) {
4992        addReplyError(c,"Invalid command specified");
4993        return;
4994    } else if (!doesCommandHaveKeys(cmd)) {
4995        addReplyError(c,"The command has no key arguments");
4996        return;
4997    } else if ((cmd->arity > 0 && cmd->arity != c->argc-2) ||
4998               ((c->argc-2) < -cmd->arity))
4999    {
5000        addReplyError(c,"Invalid number of arguments specified for command");
5001        return;
5002    }
5003
5004    if (!getKeysFromCommandWithSpecs(cmd,c->argv+2,c->argc-2,GET_KEYSPEC_DEFAULT,&result)) {
5005        if (cmd->flags & CMD_NO_MANDATORY_KEYS) {
5006            addReplyArrayLen(c,0);
5007        } else {
5008            addReplyError(c,"Invalid arguments specified for command");
5009        }
5010    } else {
5011        addReplyArrayLen(c,result.numkeys);
5012        for (j = 0; j < result.numkeys; j++) {
5013            if (!with_flags) {
5014                addReplyBulk(c,c->argv[result.keys[j].pos+2]);
5015            } else {
5016                addReplyArrayLen(c,2);
5017                addReplyBulk(c,c->argv[result.keys[j].pos+2]);
5018                addReplyFlagsForKeyArgs(c,result.keys[j].flags);
5019            }
5020        }
5021    }
5022    getKeysFreeResult(&result);
5023}
5024
5025/* COMMAND GETKEYSANDFLAGS cmd arg1 arg2 ... */
5026void commandGetKeysAndFlagsCommand(client *c) {
5027    getKeysSubcommandImpl(c, 1);
5028}
5029
5030/* COMMAND GETKEYS cmd arg1 arg2 ... */
5031void getKeysSubcommand(client *c) {
5032    getKeysSubcommandImpl(c, 0);
5033}
5034
5035/* COMMAND (no args) */
5036void commandCommand(client *c) {
5037    dictIterator *di;
5038    dictEntry *de;
5039
5040    addReplyArrayLen(c, dictSize(server.commands));
5041    di = dictGetIterator(server.commands);
5042    while ((de = dictNext(di)) != NULL) {
5043        addReplyCommandInfo(c, dictGetVal(de));
5044    }
5045    dictReleaseIterator(di);
5046}

5048/* COMMAND COUNT */
5049void commandCountCommand(client *c) {
5050    addReplyLongLong(c, dictSize(server.commands));
5051}
5052
5053typedef enum {
5054    COMMAND_LIST_FILTER_MODULE,
5055    COMMAND_LIST_FILTER_ACLCAT,
5056    COMMAND_LIST_FILTER_PATTERN,
5057} commandListFilterType;
5058
5059typedef struct {
5060    commandListFilterType type;
5061    sds arg;
5062    struct {
5063        int valid;
5064        union {
5065            uint64_t aclcat;
5066            void *module_handle;
5067        } u;
5068    } cache;
5069} commandListFilter;
5070
5071int shouldFilterFromCommandList(struct redisCommand *cmd, commandListFilter *filter) {
5072    switch (filter->type) {
5073        case (COMMAND_LIST_FILTER_MODULE):
5074            if (!filter->cache.valid) {
5075                filter->cache.u.module_handle = moduleGetHandleByName(filter->arg);
5076                filter->cache.valid = 1;
5077            }
5078            return !moduleIsModuleCommand(filter->cache.u.module_handle, cmd);
5079        case (COMMAND_LIST_FILTER_ACLCAT): {
5080            if (!filter->cache.valid) {
5081                filter->cache.u.aclcat = ACLGetCommandCategoryFlagByName(filter->arg);
5082                filter->cache.valid = 1;
5083            }
5084            uint64_t cat = filter->cache.u.aclcat;
5085            if (cat == 0)
5086                return 1; /* Invalid ACL category */
5087            return (!(cmd->acl_categories & cat));
5088            break;
5089        }
5090        case (COMMAND_LIST_FILTER_PATTERN):
5091            return !stringmatchlen(filter->arg, sdslen(filter->arg), cmd->fullname, sdslen(cmd->fullname), 1);
5092        default:
5093            serverPanic("Invalid filter type %d", filter->type);
5094    }
5095}
5096
5097/* COMMAND LIST FILTERBY (MODULE <module-name>|ACLCAT <cat>|PATTERN <pattern>) */
5098void commandListWithFilter(client *c, dict *commands, commandListFilter filter, int *numcmds) {
5099    dictEntry *de;
5100    dictIterator *di = dictGetIterator(commands);
5101
5102    while ((de = dictNext(di)) != NULL) {
5103        struct redisCommand *cmd = dictGetVal(de);
5104        if (!shouldFilterFromCommandList(cmd,&filter)) {
5105            addReplyBulkCBuffer(c, cmd->fullname, sdslen(cmd->fullname));
5106            (*numcmds)++;
5107        }
5108
5109        if (cmd->subcommands_dict) {
5110            commandListWithFilter(c, cmd->subcommands_dict, filter, numcmds);
5111        }
5112    }
5113    dictReleaseIterator(di);
5114}
5115
5116/* COMMAND LIST */
5117void commandListWithoutFilter(client *c, dict *commands, int *numcmds) {
5118    dictEntry *de;
5119    dictIterator *di = dictGetIterator(commands);
5120
5121    while ((de = dictNext(di)) != NULL) {
5122        struct redisCommand *cmd = dictGetVal(de);
5123        addReplyBulkCBuffer(c, cmd->fullname, sdslen(cmd->fullname));
5124        (*numcmds)++;
5125
5126        if (cmd->subcommands_dict) {
5127            commandListWithoutFilter(c, cmd->subcommands_dict, numcmds);
5128        }
5129    }
5130    dictReleaseIterator(di);
5131}
5132
5133/* COMMAND LIST [FILTERBY (MODULE <module-name>|ACLCAT <cat>|PATTERN <pattern>)] */
5134void commandListCommand(client *c) {
5135
5136    /* Parse options. */
5137    int i = 2, got_filter = 0;
5138    commandListFilter filter = {0};
5139    for (; i < c->argc; i++) {
5140        int moreargs = (c->argc-1) - i; /* Number of additional arguments. */
5141        char *opt = c->argv[i]->ptr;
5142        if (!strcasecmp(opt,"filterby") && moreargs == 2) {
5143            char *filtertype = c->argv[i+1]->ptr;
5144            if (!strcasecmp(filtertype,"module")) {
5145                filter.type = COMMAND_LIST_FILTER_MODULE;
5146            } else if (!strcasecmp(filtertype,"aclcat")) {
5147                filter.type = COMMAND_LIST_FILTER_ACLCAT;
5148            } else if (!strcasecmp(filtertype,"pattern")) {
5149                filter.type = COMMAND_LIST_FILTER_PATTERN;
5150            } else {
5151                addReplyErrorObject(c,shared.syntaxerr);
5152                return;
5153            }
5154            got_filter = 1;
5155            filter.arg = c->argv[i+2]->ptr;
5156            i += 2;
5157        } else {
5158            addReplyErrorObject(c,shared.syntaxerr);
5159            return;
5160        }
5161    }
5162
5163    int numcmds = 0;
5164    void *replylen = addReplyDeferredLen(c);
5165
5166    if (got_filter) {
5167        commandListWithFilter(c, server.commands, filter, &numcmds);
5168    } else {
5169        commandListWithoutFilter(c, server.commands, &numcmds);
5170    }
5171
5172    setDeferredArrayLen(c,replylen,numcmds);
5173}
5174
5175/* COMMAND INFO [<command-name> ...] */
5176void commandInfoCommand(client *c) {
5177    int i;
5178
5179    if (c->argc == 2) {
5180        dictIterator *di;
5181        dictEntry *de;
5182        addReplyArrayLen(c, dictSize(server.commands));
5183        di = dictGetIterator(server.commands);
5184        while ((de = dictNext(di)) != NULL) {
5185            addReplyCommandInfo(c, dictGetVal(de));
5186        }
5187        dictReleaseIterator(di);
5188    } else {
5189        addReplyArrayLen(c, c->argc-2);
5190        for (i = 2; i < c->argc; i++) {
5191            addReplyCommandInfo(c, lookupCommandBySds(c->argv[i]->ptr));
5192        }
5193    }
5194}
5195
5196/* COMMAND DOCS [command-name [command-name ...]] */
5197void commandDocsCommand(client *c) {
5198    int i;
5199    if (c->argc == 2) {
5200        /* Reply with an array of all commands */
5201        dictIterator *di;
5202        dictEntry *de;
5203        addReplyMapLen(c, dictSize(server.commands));
5204        di = dictGetIterator(server.commands);
5205        while ((de = dictNext(di)) != NULL) {
5206            struct redisCommand *cmd = dictGetVal(de);
5207            addReplyBulkCBuffer(c, cmd->fullname, sdslen(cmd->fullname));
5208            addReplyCommandDocs(c, cmd);
5209        }
5210        dictReleaseIterator(di);
5211    } else {
5212        /* Reply with an array of the requested commands (if we find them) */
5213        int numcmds = 0;
5214        void *replylen = addReplyDeferredLen(c);
5215        for (i = 2; i < c->argc; i++) {
5216            struct redisCommand *cmd = lookupCommandBySds(c->argv[i]->ptr);
5217            if (!cmd)
5218                continue;
5219            addReplyBulkCBuffer(c, cmd->fullname, sdslen(cmd->fullname));
5220            addReplyCommandDocs(c, cmd);
5221            numcmds++;
5222        }
5223        setDeferredMapLen(c,replylen,numcmds);
5224    }
5225}
