5259/* Convert an amount of bytes into a human readable string in the form
5260 * of 100B, 2G, 100M, 4K, and so forth. */
5261void bytesToHuman(char *s, size_t size, unsigned long long n) {
5262    double d;
5263
5264    if (n < 1024) {
5265        /* Bytes */
5266        snprintf(s,size,"%lluB",n);
5267    } else if (n < (1024*1024)) {
5268        d = (double)n/(1024);
5269        snprintf(s,size,"%.2fK",d);
5270    } else if (n < (1024LL*1024*1024)) {
5271        d = (double)n/(1024*1024);
5272        snprintf(s,size,"%.2fM",d);
5273    } else if (n < (1024LL*1024*1024*1024)) {
5274        d = (double)n/(1024LL*1024*1024);
5275        snprintf(s,size,"%.2fG",d);
5276    } else if (n < (1024LL*1024*1024*1024*1024)) {
5277        d = (double)n/(1024LL*1024*1024*1024);
5278        snprintf(s,size,"%.2fT",d);
5279    } else if (n < (1024LL*1024*1024*1024*1024*1024)) {
5280        d = (double)n/(1024LL*1024*1024*1024*1024);
5281        snprintf(s,size,"%.2fP",d);
5282    } else {
5283        /* Let's hope we never need this */
5284        snprintf(s,size,"%lluB",n);
5285    }
5286}
5287
5288/* Fill percentile distribution of latencies. */
5289sds fillPercentileDistributionLatencies(sds info, const char* histogram_name, struct hdr_histogram* histogram) {
5290    info = sdscatfmt(info,"latency_percentiles_usec_%s:",histogram_name);
5291    for (int j = 0; j < server.latency_tracking_info_percentiles_len; j++) {
5292        char fbuf[128];
5293        size_t len = snprintf(fbuf, sizeof(fbuf), "%f", server.latency_tracking_info_percentiles[j]);
5294        trimDoubleString(fbuf, len);
5295        info = sdscatprintf(info,"p%s=%.3f", fbuf,
5296            ((double)hdr_value_at_percentile(histogram,server.latency_tracking_info_percentiles[j]))/1000.0f);
5297        if (j != server.latency_tracking_info_percentiles_len-1)
5298            info = sdscatlen(info,",",1);
5299        }
5300    info = sdscatprintf(info,"\r\n");
5301    return info;
5302}
5303
5304const char *replstateToString(int replstate) {
5305    switch (replstate) {
5306    case SLAVE_STATE_WAIT_BGSAVE_START:
5307    case SLAVE_STATE_WAIT_BGSAVE_END:
5308        return "wait_bgsave";
5309    case SLAVE_STATE_SEND_BULK:
5310        return "send_bulk";
5311    case SLAVE_STATE_ONLINE:
5312        return "online";
5313    default:
5314        return "";
5315    }
5316}
5317
5318/* Characters we sanitize on INFO output to maintain expected format. */
5319static char unsafe_info_chars[] = "#:\n\r";
5320static char unsafe_info_chars_substs[] = "____";   /* Must be same length as above */
5321
5322/* Returns a sanitized version of s that contains no unsafe info string chars.
5323 * If no unsafe characters are found, simply returns s. Caller needs to
5324 * free tmp if it is non-null on return.
5325 */
5326const char *getSafeInfoString(const char *s, size_t len, char **tmp) {
5327    *tmp = NULL;
5328    if (mempbrk(s, len, unsafe_info_chars,sizeof(unsafe_info_chars)-1)
5329        == NULL) return s;
5330    char *new = *tmp = zmalloc(len + 1);
5331    memcpy(new, s, len);
5332    new[len] = '\0';
5333    return memmapchars(new, len, unsafe_info_chars, unsafe_info_chars_substs,
5334                       sizeof(unsafe_info_chars)-1);
5335}
5336
5337sds genRedisInfoStringCommandStats(sds info, dict *commands) {
5338    struct redisCommand *c;
5339    dictEntry *de;
5340    dictIterator *di;
5341    di = dictGetSafeIterator(commands);
5342    while((de = dictNext(di)) != NULL) {
5343        char *tmpsafe;
5344        c = (struct redisCommand *) dictGetVal(de);
5345        if (c->calls || c->failed_calls || c->rejected_calls) {
5346            info = sdscatprintf(info,
5347                "cmdstat_%s:calls=%lld,usec=%lld,usec_per_call=%.2f"
5348                ",rejected_calls=%lld,failed_calls=%lld\r\n",
5349                getSafeInfoString(c->fullname, sdslen(c->fullname), &tmpsafe), c->calls, c->microseconds,
5350                (c->calls == 0) ? 0 : ((float)c->microseconds/c->calls),
5351                c->rejected_calls, c->failed_calls);
5352            if (tmpsafe != NULL) zfree(tmpsafe);
5353        }
5354        if (c->subcommands_dict) {
5355            info = genRedisInfoStringCommandStats(info, c->subcommands_dict);
5356        }
5357    }
5358    dictReleaseIterator(di);
5359
5360    return info;
5361}
5362
5363/* Writes the ACL metrics to the info */
5364sds genRedisInfoStringACLStats(sds info) {
5365    info = sdscatprintf(info,
5366         "acl_access_denied_auth:%lld\r\n"
5367         "acl_access_denied_cmd:%lld\r\n"
5368         "acl_access_denied_key:%lld\r\n"
5369         "acl_access_denied_channel:%lld\r\n",
5370         server.acl_info.user_auth_failures,
5371         server.acl_info.invalid_cmd_accesses,
5372         server.acl_info.invalid_key_accesses,
5373         server.acl_info.invalid_channel_accesses);
5374    return info;
5375}
5376
5377sds genRedisInfoStringLatencyStats(sds info, dict *commands) {
5378    struct redisCommand *c;
5379    dictEntry *de;
5380    dictIterator *di;
5381    di = dictGetSafeIterator(commands);
5382    while((de = dictNext(di)) != NULL) {
5383        char *tmpsafe;
5384        c = (struct redisCommand *) dictGetVal(de);
5385        if (c->latency_histogram) {
5386            info = fillPercentileDistributionLatencies(info,
5387                getSafeInfoString(c->fullname, sdslen(c->fullname), &tmpsafe),
5388                c->latency_histogram);
5389            if (tmpsafe != NULL) zfree(tmpsafe);
5390        }
5391        if (c->subcommands_dict) {
5392            info = genRedisInfoStringLatencyStats(info, c->subcommands_dict);
5393        }
5394    }
5395    dictReleaseIterator(di);
5396
5397    return info;
5398}
5399
5400/* Takes a null terminated sections list, and adds them to the dict. */
5401void addInfoSectionsToDict(dict *section_dict, char **sections) {
5402    while (*sections) {
5403        sds section = sdsnew(*sections);
5404        if (dictAdd(section_dict, section, NULL)==DICT_ERR)
5405            sdsfree(section);
5406        sections++;
5407    }
5408}
5409
5410/* Cached copy of the default sections, as an optimization. */
5411static dict *cached_default_info_sections = NULL;
5412
5413void releaseInfoSectionDict(dict *sec) {
5414    if (sec != cached_default_info_sections)
5415        dictRelease(sec);
5416}
5417
5418/* Create a dictionary with unique section names to be used by genRedisInfoString.
5419 * 'argv' and 'argc' are list of arguments for INFO.
5420 * 'defaults' is an optional null terminated list of default sections.
5421 * 'out_all' and 'out_everything' are optional.
5422 * The resulting dictionary should be released with releaseInfoSectionDict. */
5423dict *genInfoSectionDict(robj **argv, int argc, char **defaults, int *out_all, int *out_everything) {
5424    char *default_sections[] = {
5425        "server", "clients", "memory", "persistence", "stats", "replication",
5426        "cpu", "module_list", "errorstats", "cluster", "keyspace", NULL};
5427    if (!defaults)
5428        defaults = default_sections;
5429
5430    if (argc == 0) {
5431        /* In this case we know the dict is not gonna be modified, so we cache
5432         * it as an optimization for a common case. */
5433        if (cached_default_info_sections)
5434            return cached_default_info_sections;
5435        cached_default_info_sections = dictCreate(&stringSetDictType);
5436        dictExpand(cached_default_info_sections, 16);
5437        addInfoSectionsToDict(cached_default_info_sections, defaults);
5438        return cached_default_info_sections;
5439    }
5440
5441    dict *section_dict = dictCreate(&stringSetDictType);
5442    dictExpand(section_dict, min(argc,16));
5443    for (int i = 0; i < argc; i++) {
5444        if (!strcasecmp(argv[i]->ptr,"default")) {
5445            addInfoSectionsToDict(section_dict, defaults);
5446        } else if (!strcasecmp(argv[i]->ptr,"all")) {
5447            if (out_all) *out_all = 1;
5448        } else if (!strcasecmp(argv[i]->ptr,"everything")) {
5449            if (out_everything) *out_everything = 1;
5450            if (out_all) *out_all = 1;
5451        } else {
5452            sds section = sdsnew(argv[i]->ptr);
5453            if (dictAdd(section_dict, section, NULL) != DICT_OK)
5454                sdsfree(section);
5455        }
5456    }
5457    return section_dict;
5458}
5459
5460/* Create the string returned by the INFO command. This is decoupled
5461 * by the INFO command itself as we need to report the same information
5462 * on memory corruption problems. */
5463sds genRedisInfoString(dict *section_dict, int all_sections, int everything) {
5464    sds info = sdsempty();
5465    time_t uptime = server.unixtime-server.stat_starttime;
5466    int j;
5467    int sections = 0;
5468    if (everything) all_sections = 1;
5469
5470    /* Server */
5471    if (all_sections || (dictFind(section_dict,"server") != NULL)) {
5472        static int call_uname = 1;
5473        static struct utsname name;
5474        char *mode;
5475        char *supervised;
5476
5477        if (server.cluster_enabled) mode = "cluster";
5478        else if (server.sentinel_mode) mode = "sentinel";
5479        else mode = "standalone";
5480
5481        if (server.supervised) {
5482            if (server.supervised_mode == SUPERVISED_UPSTART) supervised = "upstart";
5483            else if (server.supervised_mode == SUPERVISED_SYSTEMD) supervised = "systemd";
5484            else supervised = "unknown";
5485        } else {
5486            supervised = "no";
5487        }
5488
5489        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
5490
5491        if (call_uname) {
5492            /* Uname can be slow and is always the same output. Cache it. */
5493            uname(&name);
5494            call_uname = 0;
5495        }
5496
5497        info = sdscatfmt(info,
5498            "# Server\r\n"
5499            "redis_version:%s\r\n"
5500            "redis_git_sha1:%s\r\n"
5501            "redis_git_dirty:%i\r\n"
5502            "redis_build_id:%s\r\n"
5503            "redis_mode:%s\r\n"
5504            "os:%s %s %s\r\n"
5505            "arch_bits:%i\r\n"
5506            "monotonic_clock:%s\r\n"
5507            "multiplexing_api:%s\r\n"
5508            "atomicvar_api:%s\r\n"
5509            "gcc_version:%i.%i.%i\r\n"
5510            "process_id:%I\r\n"
5511            "process_supervised:%s\r\n"
5512            "run_id:%s\r\n"
5513            "tcp_port:%i\r\n"
5514            "server_time_usec:%I\r\n"
5515            "uptime_in_seconds:%I\r\n"
5516            "uptime_in_days:%I\r\n"
5517            "hz:%i\r\n"
5518            "configured_hz:%i\r\n"
5519            "lru_clock:%u\r\n"
5520            "executable:%s\r\n"
5521            "config_file:%s\r\n"
5522            "io_threads_active:%i\r\n",
5523            REDIS_VERSION,
5524            redisGitSHA1(),
5525            strtol(redisGitDirty(),NULL,10) > 0,
5526            redisBuildIdString(),
5527            mode,
5528            name.sysname, name.release, name.machine,
5529            server.arch_bits,
5530            monotonicInfoString(),
5531            aeGetApiName(),
5532            REDIS_ATOMIC_API,
5533#ifdef __GNUC__
5534            __GNUC__,__GNUC_MINOR__,__GNUC_PATCHLEVEL__,
5535#else
5536            0,0,0,
5537#endif
5538            (int64_t) getpid(),
5539            supervised,
5540            server.runid,
5541            server.port ? server.port : server.tls_port,
5542            (int64_t)server.ustime,
5543            (int64_t)uptime,
5544            (int64_t)(uptime/(3600*24)),
5545            server.hz,
5546            server.config_hz,
5547            server.lruclock,
5548            server.executable ? server.executable : "",
5549            server.configfile ? server.configfile : "",
5550            server.io_threads_active);
5551
5552        /* Conditional properties */
5553        if (isShutdownInitiated()) {
5554            info = sdscatfmt(info,
5555                "shutdown_in_milliseconds:%I\r\n",
5556                (int64_t)(server.shutdown_mstime - commandTimeSnapshot()));
5557        }
5558
5559        /* get all the listeners information */
5560        info = getListensInfoString(info);
5561    }
5562
5563    /* Clients */
5564    if (all_sections || (dictFind(section_dict,"clients") != NULL)) {
5565        size_t maxin, maxout;
5566        unsigned long blocking_keys, blocking_keys_on_nokey;
5567        getExpansiveClientsInfo(&maxin,&maxout);
5568        totalNumberOfBlockingKeys(&blocking_keys, &blocking_keys_on_nokey);
5569        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
5570        info = sdscatprintf(info,
5571            "# Clients\r\n"
5572            "connected_clients:%lu\r\n"
5573            "cluster_connections:%lu\r\n"
5574            "maxclients:%u\r\n"
5575            "client_recent_max_input_buffer:%zu\r\n"
5576            "client_recent_max_output_buffer:%zu\r\n"
5577            "blocked_clients:%d\r\n"
5578            "tracking_clients:%d\r\n"
5579            "clients_in_timeout_table:%llu\r\n"
5580            "total_blocking_keys:%lu\r\n"
5581            "total_blocking_keys_on_nokey:%lu\r\n",
5582            listLength(server.clients)-listLength(server.slaves),
5583            getClusterConnectionsCount(),
5584            server.maxclients,
5585            maxin, maxout,
5586            server.blocked_clients,
5587            server.tracking_clients,
5588            (unsigned long long) raxSize(server.clients_timeout_table),
5589            blocking_keys,
5590            blocking_keys_on_nokey);
5591    }
5592
5593    /* Memory */
5594    if (all_sections || (dictFind(section_dict,"memory") != NULL)) {
5595        char hmem[64];
5596        char peak_hmem[64];
5597        char total_system_hmem[64];
5598        char used_memory_lua_hmem[64];
5599        char used_memory_vm_total_hmem[64];
5600        char used_memory_scripts_hmem[64];
5601        char used_memory_rss_hmem[64];
5602        char maxmemory_hmem[64];
5603        size_t zmalloc_used = zmalloc_used_memory();
5604        size_t total_system_mem = server.system_memory_size;
5605        const char *evict_policy = evictPolicyToString();
5606        long long memory_lua = evalMemory();
5607        long long memory_functions = functionsMemory();
5608        struct redisMemOverhead *mh = getMemoryOverheadData();
5609
5610        /* Peak memory is updated from time to time by serverCron() so it
5611         * may happen that the instantaneous value is slightly bigger than
5612         * the peak value. This may confuse users, so we update the peak
5613         * if found smaller than the current memory usage. */
5614        if (zmalloc_used > server.stat_peak_memory)
5615            server.stat_peak_memory = zmalloc_used;
5616
5617        bytesToHuman(hmem,sizeof(hmem),zmalloc_used);
5618        bytesToHuman(peak_hmem,sizeof(peak_hmem),server.stat_peak_memory);
5619        bytesToHuman(total_system_hmem,sizeof(total_system_hmem),total_system_mem);
5620        bytesToHuman(used_memory_lua_hmem,sizeof(used_memory_lua_hmem),memory_lua);
5621        bytesToHuman(used_memory_vm_total_hmem,sizeof(used_memory_vm_total_hmem),memory_functions + memory_lua);
5622        bytesToHuman(used_memory_scripts_hmem,sizeof(used_memory_scripts_hmem),mh->lua_caches + mh->functions_caches);
5623        bytesToHuman(used_memory_rss_hmem,sizeof(used_memory_rss_hmem),server.cron_malloc_stats.process_rss);
5624        bytesToHuman(maxmemory_hmem,sizeof(maxmemory_hmem),server.maxmemory);
5625
5626        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
5627        info = sdscatprintf(info,
5628            "# Memory\r\n"
5629            "used_memory:%zu\r\n"
5630            "used_memory_human:%s\r\n"
5631            "used_memory_rss:%zu\r\n"
5632            "used_memory_rss_human:%s\r\n"
5633            "used_memory_peak:%zu\r\n"
5634            "used_memory_peak_human:%s\r\n"
5635            "used_memory_peak_perc:%.2f%%\r\n"
5636            "used_memory_overhead:%zu\r\n"
5637            "used_memory_startup:%zu\r\n"
5638            "used_memory_dataset:%zu\r\n"
5639            "used_memory_dataset_perc:%.2f%%\r\n"
5640            "allocator_allocated:%zu\r\n"
5641            "allocator_active:%zu\r\n"
5642            "allocator_resident:%zu\r\n"
5643            "total_system_memory:%lu\r\n"
5644            "total_system_memory_human:%s\r\n"
5645            "used_memory_lua:%lld\r\n" /* deprecated, renamed to used_memory_vm_eval */
5646            "used_memory_vm_eval:%lld\r\n"
5647            "used_memory_lua_human:%s\r\n" /* deprecated */
5648            "used_memory_scripts_eval:%lld\r\n"
5649            "number_of_cached_scripts:%lu\r\n"
5650            "number_of_functions:%lu\r\n"
5651            "number_of_libraries:%lu\r\n"
5652            "used_memory_vm_functions:%lld\r\n"
5653            "used_memory_vm_total:%lld\r\n"
5654            "used_memory_vm_total_human:%s\r\n"
5655            "used_memory_functions:%lld\r\n"
5656            "used_memory_scripts:%lld\r\n"
5657            "used_memory_scripts_human:%s\r\n"
5658            "maxmemory:%lld\r\n"
5659            "maxmemory_human:%s\r\n"
5660            "maxmemory_policy:%s\r\n"
5661            "allocator_frag_ratio:%.2f\r\n"
5662            "allocator_frag_bytes:%zu\r\n"
5663            "allocator_rss_ratio:%.2f\r\n"
5664            "allocator_rss_bytes:%zd\r\n"
5665            "rss_overhead_ratio:%.2f\r\n"
5666            "rss_overhead_bytes:%zd\r\n"
5667            "mem_fragmentation_ratio:%.2f\r\n"
5668            "mem_fragmentation_bytes:%zd\r\n"
5669            "mem_not_counted_for_evict:%zu\r\n"
5670            "mem_replication_backlog:%zu\r\n"
5671            "mem_total_replication_buffers:%zu\r\n"
5672            "mem_clients_slaves:%zu\r\n"
5673            "mem_clients_normal:%zu\r\n"
5674            "mem_cluster_links:%zu\r\n"
5675            "mem_aof_buffer:%zu\r\n"
5676            "mem_allocator:%s\r\n"
5677            "active_defrag_running:%d\r\n"
5678            "lazyfree_pending_objects:%zu\r\n"
5679            "lazyfreed_objects:%zu\r\n",
5680            zmalloc_used,
5681            hmem,
5682            server.cron_malloc_stats.process_rss,
5683            used_memory_rss_hmem,
5684            server.stat_peak_memory,
5685            peak_hmem,
5686            mh->peak_perc,
5687            mh->overhead_total,
5688            mh->startup_allocated,
5689            mh->dataset,
5690            mh->dataset_perc,
5691            server.cron_malloc_stats.allocator_allocated,
5692            server.cron_malloc_stats.allocator_active,
5693            server.cron_malloc_stats.allocator_resident,
5694            (unsigned long)total_system_mem,
5695            total_system_hmem,
5696            memory_lua,
5697            memory_lua,
5698            used_memory_lua_hmem,
5699            (long long) mh->lua_caches,
5700            dictSize(evalScriptsDict()),
5701            functionsNum(),
5702            functionsLibNum(),
5703            memory_functions,
5704            memory_functions + memory_lua,
5705            used_memory_vm_total_hmem,
5706            (long long) mh->functions_caches,
5707            (long long) mh->lua_caches + (long long) mh->functions_caches,
5708            used_memory_scripts_hmem,
5709            server.maxmemory,
5710            maxmemory_hmem,
5711            evict_policy,
5712            mh->allocator_frag,
5713            mh->allocator_frag_bytes,
5714            mh->allocator_rss,
5715            mh->allocator_rss_bytes,
5716            mh->rss_extra,
5717            mh->rss_extra_bytes,
5718            mh->total_frag,       /* This is the total RSS overhead, including
5719                                     fragmentation, but not just it. This field
5720                                     (and the next one) is named like that just
5721                                     for backward compatibility. */
5722            mh->total_frag_bytes,
5723            freeMemoryGetNotCountedMemory(),
5724            mh->repl_backlog,
5725            server.repl_buffer_mem,
5726            mh->clients_slaves,
5727            mh->clients_normal,
5728            mh->cluster_links,
5729            mh->aof_buffer,
5730            ZMALLOC_LIB,
5731            server.active_defrag_running,
5732            lazyfreeGetPendingObjectsCount(),
5733            lazyfreeGetFreedObjectsCount()
5734        );
5735        freeMemoryOverheadData(mh);
5736    }
5737
5738    /* Persistence */
5739    if (all_sections || (dictFind(section_dict,"persistence") != NULL)) {
5740        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
5741        double fork_perc = 0;
5742        if (server.stat_module_progress) {
5743            fork_perc = server.stat_module_progress * 100;
5744        } else if (server.stat_current_save_keys_total) {
5745            fork_perc = ((double)server.stat_current_save_keys_processed / server.stat_current_save_keys_total) * 100;
5746        }
5747        int aof_bio_fsync_status;
5748        atomicGet(server.aof_bio_fsync_status,aof_bio_fsync_status);
5749
5750        info = sdscatprintf(info,
5751            "# Persistence\r\n"
5752            "loading:%d\r\n"
5753            "async_loading:%d\r\n"
5754            "current_cow_peak:%zu\r\n"
5755            "current_cow_size:%zu\r\n"
5756            "current_cow_size_age:%lu\r\n"
5757            "current_fork_perc:%.2f\r\n"
5758            "current_save_keys_processed:%zu\r\n"
5759            "current_save_keys_total:%zu\r\n"
5760            "rdb_changes_since_last_save:%lld\r\n"
5761            "rdb_bgsave_in_progress:%d\r\n"
5762            "rdb_last_save_time:%jd\r\n"
5763            "rdb_last_bgsave_status:%s\r\n"
5764            "rdb_last_bgsave_time_sec:%jd\r\n"
5765            "rdb_current_bgsave_time_sec:%jd\r\n"
5766            "rdb_saves:%lld\r\n"
5767            "rdb_last_cow_size:%zu\r\n"
5768            "rdb_last_load_keys_expired:%lld\r\n"
5769            "rdb_last_load_keys_loaded:%lld\r\n"
5770            "aof_enabled:%d\r\n"
5771            "aof_rewrite_in_progress:%d\r\n"
5772            "aof_rewrite_scheduled:%d\r\n"
5773            "aof_last_rewrite_time_sec:%jd\r\n"
5774            "aof_current_rewrite_time_sec:%jd\r\n"
5775            "aof_last_bgrewrite_status:%s\r\n"
5776            "aof_rewrites:%lld\r\n"
5777            "aof_rewrites_consecutive_failures:%lld\r\n"
5778            "aof_last_write_status:%s\r\n"
5779            "aof_last_cow_size:%zu\r\n"
5780            "module_fork_in_progress:%d\r\n"
5781            "module_fork_last_cow_size:%zu\r\n",
5782            (int)(server.loading && !server.async_loading),
5783            (int)server.async_loading,
5784            server.stat_current_cow_peak,
5785            server.stat_current_cow_bytes,
5786            server.stat_current_cow_updated ? (unsigned long) elapsedMs(server.stat_current_cow_updated) / 1000 : 0,
5787            fork_perc,
5788            server.stat_current_save_keys_processed,
5789            server.stat_current_save_keys_total,
5790            server.dirty,
5791            server.child_type == CHILD_TYPE_RDB,
5792            (intmax_t)server.lastsave,
5793            (server.lastbgsave_status == C_OK) ? "ok" : "err",
5794            (intmax_t)server.rdb_save_time_last,
5795            (intmax_t)((server.child_type != CHILD_TYPE_RDB) ?
5796                -1 : time(NULL)-server.rdb_save_time_start),
5797            server.stat_rdb_saves,
5798            server.stat_rdb_cow_bytes,
5799            server.rdb_last_load_keys_expired,
5800            server.rdb_last_load_keys_loaded,
5801            server.aof_state != AOF_OFF,
5802            server.child_type == CHILD_TYPE_AOF,
5803            server.aof_rewrite_scheduled,
5804            (intmax_t)server.aof_rewrite_time_last,
5805            (intmax_t)((server.child_type != CHILD_TYPE_AOF) ?
5806                -1 : time(NULL)-server.aof_rewrite_time_start),
5807            (server.aof_lastbgrewrite_status == C_OK) ? "ok" : "err",
5808            server.stat_aof_rewrites,
5809            server.stat_aofrw_consecutive_failures,
5810            (server.aof_last_write_status == C_OK &&
5811                aof_bio_fsync_status == C_OK) ? "ok" : "err",
5812            server.stat_aof_cow_bytes,
5813            server.child_type == CHILD_TYPE_MODULE,
5814            server.stat_module_cow_bytes);
5815
5816        if (server.aof_enabled) {
5817            info = sdscatprintf(info,
5818                "aof_current_size:%lld\r\n"
5819                "aof_base_size:%lld\r\n"
5820                "aof_pending_rewrite:%d\r\n"
5821                "aof_buffer_length:%zu\r\n"
5822                "aof_pending_bio_fsync:%lu\r\n"
5823                "aof_delayed_fsync:%lu\r\n",
5824                (long long) server.aof_current_size,
5825                (long long) server.aof_rewrite_base_size,
5826                server.aof_rewrite_scheduled,
5827                sdslen(server.aof_buf),
5828                bioPendingJobsOfType(BIO_AOF_FSYNC),
5829                server.aof_delayed_fsync);
5830        }
5831
5832        if (server.loading) {
5833            double perc = 0;
5834            time_t eta, elapsed;
5835            off_t remaining_bytes = 1;
5836
5837            if (server.loading_total_bytes) {
5838                perc = ((double)server.loading_loaded_bytes / server.loading_total_bytes) * 100;
5839                remaining_bytes = server.loading_total_bytes - server.loading_loaded_bytes;
5840            } else if(server.loading_rdb_used_mem) {
5841                perc = ((double)server.loading_loaded_bytes / server.loading_rdb_used_mem) * 100;
5842                remaining_bytes = server.loading_rdb_used_mem - server.loading_loaded_bytes;
5843                /* used mem is only a (bad) estimation of the rdb file size, avoid going over 100% */
5844                if (perc > 99.99) perc = 99.99;
5845                if (remaining_bytes < 1) remaining_bytes = 1;
5846            }
5847
5848            elapsed = time(NULL)-server.loading_start_time;
5849            if (elapsed == 0) {
5850                eta = 1; /* A fake 1 second figure if we don't have
5851                            enough info */
5852            } else {
5853                eta = (elapsed*remaining_bytes)/(server.loading_loaded_bytes+1);
5854            }
5855
5856            info = sdscatprintf(info,
5857                "loading_start_time:%jd\r\n"
5858                "loading_total_bytes:%llu\r\n"
5859                "loading_rdb_used_mem:%llu\r\n"
5860                "loading_loaded_bytes:%llu\r\n"
5861                "loading_loaded_perc:%.2f\r\n"
5862                "loading_eta_seconds:%jd\r\n",
5863                (intmax_t) server.loading_start_time,
5864                (unsigned long long) server.loading_total_bytes,
5865                (unsigned long long) server.loading_rdb_used_mem,
5866                (unsigned long long) server.loading_loaded_bytes,
5867                perc,
5868                (intmax_t)eta
5869            );
5870        }
5871    }
5872
5873    /* Stats */
5874    if (all_sections  || (dictFind(section_dict,"stats") != NULL)) {
5875        long long stat_total_reads_processed, stat_total_writes_processed;
5876        long long stat_net_input_bytes, stat_net_output_bytes;
5877        long long stat_net_repl_input_bytes, stat_net_repl_output_bytes;
5878        long long current_eviction_exceeded_time = server.stat_last_eviction_exceeded_time ?
5879            (long long) elapsedUs(server.stat_last_eviction_exceeded_time): 0;
5880        long long current_active_defrag_time = server.stat_last_active_defrag_time ?
5881            (long long) elapsedUs(server.stat_last_active_defrag_time): 0;
5882        atomicGet(server.stat_total_reads_processed, stat_total_reads_processed);
5883        atomicGet(server.stat_total_writes_processed, stat_total_writes_processed);
5884        atomicGet(server.stat_net_input_bytes, stat_net_input_bytes);
5885        atomicGet(server.stat_net_output_bytes, stat_net_output_bytes);
5886        atomicGet(server.stat_net_repl_input_bytes, stat_net_repl_input_bytes);
5887        atomicGet(server.stat_net_repl_output_bytes, stat_net_repl_output_bytes);
5888
5889        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
5890        info = sdscatprintf(info,
5891            "# Stats\r\n"
5892            "total_connections_received:%lld\r\n"
5893            "total_commands_processed:%lld\r\n"
5894            "instantaneous_ops_per_sec:%lld\r\n"
5895            "total_net_input_bytes:%lld\r\n"
5896            "total_net_output_bytes:%lld\r\n"
5897            "total_net_repl_input_bytes:%lld\r\n"
5898            "total_net_repl_output_bytes:%lld\r\n"
5899            "instantaneous_input_kbps:%.2f\r\n"
5900            "instantaneous_output_kbps:%.2f\r\n"
5901            "instantaneous_input_repl_kbps:%.2f\r\n"
5902            "instantaneous_output_repl_kbps:%.2f\r\n"
5903            "rejected_connections:%lld\r\n"
5904            "sync_full:%lld\r\n"
5905            "sync_partial_ok:%lld\r\n"
5906            "sync_partial_err:%lld\r\n"
5907            "expired_keys:%lld\r\n"
5908            "expired_stale_perc:%.2f\r\n"
5909            "expired_time_cap_reached_count:%lld\r\n"
5910            "expire_cycle_cpu_milliseconds:%lld\r\n"
5911            "evicted_keys:%lld\r\n"
5912            "evicted_clients:%lld\r\n"
5913            "total_eviction_exceeded_time:%lld\r\n"
5914            "current_eviction_exceeded_time:%lld\r\n"
5915            "keyspace_hits:%lld\r\n"
5916            "keyspace_misses:%lld\r\n"
5917            "pubsub_channels:%ld\r\n"
5918            "pubsub_patterns:%lu\r\n"
5919            "pubsubshard_channels:%lu\r\n"
5920            "latest_fork_usec:%lld\r\n"
5921            "total_forks:%lld\r\n"
5922            "migrate_cached_sockets:%ld\r\n"
5923            "slave_expires_tracked_keys:%zu\r\n"
5924            "active_defrag_hits:%lld\r\n"
5925            "active_defrag_misses:%lld\r\n"
5926            "active_defrag_key_hits:%lld\r\n"
5927            "active_defrag_key_misses:%lld\r\n"
5928            "total_active_defrag_time:%lld\r\n"
5929            "current_active_defrag_time:%lld\r\n"
5930            "tracking_total_keys:%lld\r\n"
5931            "tracking_total_items:%lld\r\n"
5932            "tracking_total_prefixes:%lld\r\n"
5933            "unexpected_error_replies:%lld\r\n"
5934            "total_error_replies:%lld\r\n"
5935            "dump_payload_sanitizations:%lld\r\n"
5936            "total_reads_processed:%lld\r\n"
5937            "total_writes_processed:%lld\r\n"
5938            "io_threaded_reads_processed:%lld\r\n"
5939            "io_threaded_writes_processed:%lld\r\n"
5940            "reply_buffer_shrinks:%lld\r\n"
5941            "reply_buffer_expands:%lld\r\n"
5942            "eventloop_cycles:%llu\r\n"
5943            "eventloop_duration_sum:%llu\r\n"
5944            "eventloop_duration_cmd_sum:%llu\r\n"
5945            "instantaneous_eventloop_cycles_per_sec:%llu\r\n"
5946            "instantaneous_eventloop_duration_usec:%llu\r\n",
5947            server.stat_numconnections,
5948            server.stat_numcommands,
5949            getInstantaneousMetric(STATS_METRIC_COMMAND),
5950            stat_net_input_bytes + stat_net_repl_input_bytes,
5951            stat_net_output_bytes + stat_net_repl_output_bytes,
5952            stat_net_repl_input_bytes,
5953            stat_net_repl_output_bytes,
5954            (float)getInstantaneousMetric(STATS_METRIC_NET_INPUT)/1024,
5955            (float)getInstantaneousMetric(STATS_METRIC_NET_OUTPUT)/1024,
5956            (float)getInstantaneousMetric(STATS_METRIC_NET_INPUT_REPLICATION)/1024,
5957            (float)getInstantaneousMetric(STATS_METRIC_NET_OUTPUT_REPLICATION)/1024,
5958            server.stat_rejected_conn,
5959            server.stat_sync_full,
5960            server.stat_sync_partial_ok,
5961            server.stat_sync_partial_err,
5962            server.stat_expiredkeys,
5963            server.stat_expired_stale_perc*100,
5964            server.stat_expired_time_cap_reached_count,
5965            server.stat_expire_cycle_time_used/1000,
5966            server.stat_evictedkeys,
5967            server.stat_evictedclients,
5968            (server.stat_total_eviction_exceeded_time + current_eviction_exceeded_time) / 1000,
5969            current_eviction_exceeded_time / 1000,
5970            server.stat_keyspace_hits,
5971            server.stat_keyspace_misses,
5972            dictSize(server.pubsub_channels),
5973            dictSize(server.pubsub_patterns),
5974            dictSize(server.pubsubshard_channels),
5975            server.stat_fork_time,
5976            server.stat_total_forks,
5977            dictSize(server.migrate_cached_sockets),
5978            getSlaveKeyWithExpireCount(),
5979            server.stat_active_defrag_hits,
5980            server.stat_active_defrag_misses,
5981            server.stat_active_defrag_key_hits,
5982            server.stat_active_defrag_key_misses,
5983            (server.stat_total_active_defrag_time + current_active_defrag_time) / 1000,
5984            current_active_defrag_time / 1000,
5985            (unsigned long long) trackingGetTotalKeys(),
5986            (unsigned long long) trackingGetTotalItems(),
5987            (unsigned long long) trackingGetTotalPrefixes(),
5988            server.stat_unexpected_error_replies,
5989            server.stat_total_error_replies,
5990            server.stat_dump_payload_sanitizations,
5991            stat_total_reads_processed,
5992            stat_total_writes_processed,
5993            server.stat_io_reads_processed,
5994            server.stat_io_writes_processed,
5995            server.stat_reply_buffer_shrinks,
5996            server.stat_reply_buffer_expands,
5997            server.duration_stats[EL_DURATION_TYPE_EL].cnt,
5998            server.duration_stats[EL_DURATION_TYPE_EL].sum,
5999            server.duration_stats[EL_DURATION_TYPE_CMD].sum,
6000            getInstantaneousMetric(STATS_METRIC_EL_CYCLE),
6001            getInstantaneousMetric(STATS_METRIC_EL_DURATION));
6002        info = genRedisInfoStringACLStats(info);
6003    }
6004
6005    /* Replication */
6006    if (all_sections || (dictFind(section_dict,"replication") != NULL)) {
6007        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
6008        info = sdscatprintf(info,
6009            "# Replication\r\n"
6010            "role:%s\r\n",
6011            server.masterhost == NULL ? "master" : "slave");
6012        if (server.masterhost) {
6013            long long slave_repl_offset = 1;
6014            long long slave_read_repl_offset = 1;
6015
6016            if (server.master) {
6017                slave_repl_offset = server.master->reploff;
6018                slave_read_repl_offset = server.master->read_reploff;
6019            } else if (server.cached_master) {
6020                slave_repl_offset = server.cached_master->reploff;
6021                slave_read_repl_offset = server.cached_master->read_reploff;
6022            }
6023
6024            info = sdscatprintf(info,
6025                "master_host:%s\r\n"
6026                "master_port:%d\r\n"
6027                "master_link_status:%s\r\n"
6028                "master_last_io_seconds_ago:%d\r\n"
6029                "master_sync_in_progress:%d\r\n"
6030                "slave_read_repl_offset:%lld\r\n"
6031                "slave_repl_offset:%lld\r\n"
6032                ,server.masterhost,
6033                server.masterport,
6034                (server.repl_state == REPL_STATE_CONNECTED) ?
6035                    "up" : "down",
6036                server.master ?
6037                ((int)(server.unixtime-server.master->lastinteraction)) : -1,
6038                server.repl_state == REPL_STATE_TRANSFER,
6039                slave_read_repl_offset,
6040                slave_repl_offset
6041            );
6042
6043            if (server.repl_state == REPL_STATE_TRANSFER) {
6044                double perc = 0;
6045                if (server.repl_transfer_size) {
6046                    perc = ((double)server.repl_transfer_read / server.repl_transfer_size) * 100;
6047                }
6048                info = sdscatprintf(info,
6049                    "master_sync_total_bytes:%lld\r\n"
6050                    "master_sync_read_bytes:%lld\r\n"
6051                    "master_sync_left_bytes:%lld\r\n"
6052                    "master_sync_perc:%.2f\r\n"
6053                    "master_sync_last_io_seconds_ago:%d\r\n",
6054                    (long long) server.repl_transfer_size,
6055                    (long long) server.repl_transfer_read,
6056                    (long long) (server.repl_transfer_size - server.repl_transfer_read),
6057                    perc,
6058                    (int)(server.unixtime-server.repl_transfer_lastio)
6059                );
6060            }
6061
6062            if (server.repl_state != REPL_STATE_CONNECTED) {
6063                info = sdscatprintf(info,
6064                    "master_link_down_since_seconds:%jd\r\n",
6065                    server.repl_down_since ?
6066                    (intmax_t)(server.unixtime-server.repl_down_since) : -1);
6067            }
6068            info = sdscatprintf(info,
6069                "slave_priority:%d\r\n"
6070                "slave_read_only:%d\r\n"
6071                "replica_announced:%d\r\n",
6072                server.slave_priority,
6073                server.repl_slave_ro,
6074                server.replica_announced);
6075        }
6076
6077        info = sdscatprintf(info,
6078            "connected_slaves:%lu\r\n",
6079            listLength(server.slaves));
6080
6081        /* If min-slaves-to-write is active, write the number of slaves
6082         * currently considered 'good'. */
6083        if (server.repl_min_slaves_to_write &&
6084            server.repl_min_slaves_max_lag) {
6085            info = sdscatprintf(info,
6086                "min_slaves_good_slaves:%d\r\n",
6087                server.repl_good_slaves_count);
6088        }
6089
6090        if (listLength(server.slaves)) {
6091            int slaveid = 0;
6092            listNode *ln;
6093            listIter li;
6094
6095            listRewind(server.slaves,&li);
6096            while((ln = listNext(&li))) {
6097                client *slave = listNodeValue(ln);
6098                char ip[NET_IP_STR_LEN], *slaveip = slave->slave_addr;
6099                int port;
6100                long lag = 0;
6101
6102                if (!slaveip) {
6103                    if (connAddrPeerName(slave->conn,ip,sizeof(ip),&port) == -1)
6104                        continue;
6105                    slaveip = ip;
6106                }
6107                const char *state = replstateToString(slave->replstate);
6108                if (state[0] == '\0') continue;
6109                if (slave->replstate == SLAVE_STATE_ONLINE)
6110                    lag = time(NULL) - slave->repl_ack_time;
6111
6112                info = sdscatprintf(info,
6113                    "slave%d:ip=%s,port=%d,state=%s,"
6114                    "offset=%lld,lag=%ld\r\n",
6115                    slaveid,slaveip,slave->slave_listening_port,state,
6116                    slave->repl_ack_off, lag);
6117                slaveid++;
6118            }
6119        }
6120        info = sdscatprintf(info,
6121            "master_failover_state:%s\r\n"
6122            "master_replid:%s\r\n"
6123            "master_replid2:%s\r\n"
6124            "master_repl_offset:%lld\r\n"
6125            "second_repl_offset:%lld\r\n"
6126            "repl_backlog_active:%d\r\n"
6127            "repl_backlog_size:%lld\r\n"
6128            "repl_backlog_first_byte_offset:%lld\r\n"
6129            "repl_backlog_histlen:%lld\r\n",
6130            getFailoverStateString(),
6131            server.replid,
6132            server.replid2,
6133            server.master_repl_offset,
6134            server.second_replid_offset,
6135            server.repl_backlog != NULL,
6136            server.repl_backlog_size,
6137            server.repl_backlog ? server.repl_backlog->offset : 0,
6138            server.repl_backlog ? server.repl_backlog->histlen : 0);
6139    }
6140
6141    /* CPU */
6142    if (all_sections || (dictFind(section_dict,"cpu") != NULL)) {
6143        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
6144
6145        struct rusage self_ru, c_ru;
6146        getrusage(RUSAGE_SELF, &self_ru);
6147        getrusage(RUSAGE_CHILDREN, &c_ru);
6148        info = sdscatprintf(info,
6149        "# CPU\r\n"
6150        "used_cpu_sys:%ld.%06ld\r\n"
6151        "used_cpu_user:%ld.%06ld\r\n"
6152        "used_cpu_sys_children:%ld.%06ld\r\n"
6153        "used_cpu_user_children:%ld.%06ld\r\n",
6154        (long)self_ru.ru_stime.tv_sec, (long)self_ru.ru_stime.tv_usec,
6155        (long)self_ru.ru_utime.tv_sec, (long)self_ru.ru_utime.tv_usec,
6156        (long)c_ru.ru_stime.tv_sec, (long)c_ru.ru_stime.tv_usec,
6157        (long)c_ru.ru_utime.tv_sec, (long)c_ru.ru_utime.tv_usec);
6158#ifdef RUSAGE_THREAD
6159        struct rusage m_ru;
6160        getrusage(RUSAGE_THREAD, &m_ru);
6161        info = sdscatprintf(info,
6162            "used_cpu_sys_main_thread:%ld.%06ld\r\n"
6163            "used_cpu_user_main_thread:%ld.%06ld\r\n",
6164            (long)m_ru.ru_stime.tv_sec, (long)m_ru.ru_stime.tv_usec,
6165            (long)m_ru.ru_utime.tv_sec, (long)m_ru.ru_utime.tv_usec);
6166#endif  /* RUSAGE_THREAD */
6167    }
6168
6169    /* Modules */
6170    if (all_sections || (dictFind(section_dict,"module_list") != NULL) || (dictFind(section_dict,"modules") != NULL)) {
6171        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
6172        info = sdscatprintf(info,"# Modules\r\n");
6173        info = genModulesInfoString(info);
6174    }
6175
6176    /* Command statistics */
6177    if (all_sections || (dictFind(section_dict,"commandstats") != NULL)) {
6178        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
6179        info = sdscatprintf(info, "# Commandstats\r\n");
6180        info = genRedisInfoStringCommandStats(info, server.commands);
6181    }
6182
6183    /* Error statistics */
6184    if (all_sections || (dictFind(section_dict,"errorstats") != NULL)) {
6185        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
6186        info = sdscat(info, "# Errorstats\r\n");
6187        raxIterator ri;
6188        raxStart(&ri,server.errors);
6189        raxSeek(&ri,"^",NULL,0);
6190        struct redisError *e;
6191        while(raxNext(&ri)) {
6192            char *tmpsafe;
6193            e = (struct redisError *) ri.data;
6194            info = sdscatprintf(info,
6195                "errorstat_%.*s:count=%lld\r\n",
6196                (int)ri.key_len, getSafeInfoString((char *) ri.key, ri.key_len, &tmpsafe), e->count);
6197            if (tmpsafe != NULL) zfree(tmpsafe);
6198        }
6199        raxStop(&ri);
6200    }
6201
6202    /* Latency by percentile distribution per command */
6203    if (all_sections || (dictFind(section_dict,"latencystats") != NULL)) {
6204        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
6205        info = sdscatprintf(info, "# Latencystats\r\n");
6206        if (server.latency_tracking_enabled) {
6207            info = genRedisInfoStringLatencyStats(info, server.commands);
6208        }
6209    }
6210
6211    /* Cluster */
6212    if (all_sections || (dictFind(section_dict,"cluster") != NULL)) {
6213        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
6214        info = sdscatprintf(info,
6215        "# Cluster\r\n"
6216        "cluster_enabled:%d\r\n",
6217        server.cluster_enabled);
6218    }
6219
6220    /* Key space */
6221    if (all_sections || (dictFind(section_dict,"keyspace") != NULL)) {
6222        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
6223        info = sdscatprintf(info, "# Keyspace\r\n");
6224        for (j = 0; j < server.dbnum; j++) {
6225            long long keys, vkeys;
6226
6227            keys = dictSize(server.db[j].dict);
6228            vkeys = dictSize(server.db[j].expires);
6229            if (keys || vkeys) {
6230                info = sdscatprintf(info,
6231                    "db%d:keys=%lld,expires=%lld,avg_ttl=%lld\r\n",
6232                    j, keys, vkeys, server.db[j].avg_ttl);
6233            }
6234        }
6235    }
6236
6237    /* Get info from modules.
6238     * Returned when the user asked for "everything", "modules", or a specific module section.
6239     * We're not aware of the module section names here, and we rather avoid the search when we can.
6240     * so we proceed if there's a requested section name that's not found yet, or when the user asked
6241     * for "all" with any additional section names. */
6242    if (everything || dictFind(section_dict, "modules") != NULL || sections < (int)dictSize(section_dict) ||
6243        (all_sections && dictSize(section_dict)))
6244    {
6245
6246        info = modulesCollectInfo(info,
6247                                  everything || dictFind(section_dict, "modules") != NULL ? NULL: section_dict,
6248                                  0, /* not a crash report */
6249                                  sections);
6250    }
6251
6252    if (dictFind(section_dict, "debug") != NULL) {
6253        if (sections++) info = sdscat(info,"\r\n");
6254        info = sdscatprintf(info,
6255        "# Debug\r\n"
6256        "eventloop_duration_aof_sum:%llu\r\n"
6257        "eventloop_duration_cron_sum:%llu\r\n"
6258        "eventloop_duration_max:%llu\r\n"
6259        "eventloop_cmd_per_cycle_max:%lld\r\n",
6260        server.duration_stats[EL_DURATION_TYPE_AOF].sum,
6261        server.duration_stats[EL_DURATION_TYPE_CRON].sum,
6262        server.duration_stats[EL_DURATION_TYPE_EL].max,
6263        server.el_cmd_cnt_max);
6264    }
6265
6266    return info;
6267}
6268
6269/* INFO [<section> [<section> ...]] */
6270void infoCommand(client *c) {
6271    if (server.sentinel_mode) {
6272        sentinelInfoCommand(c);
6273        return;
6274    }
6275    int all_sections = 0;
6276    int everything = 0;
6277    dict *sections_dict = genInfoSectionDict(c->argv+1, c->argc-1, NULL, &all_sections, &everything);
6278    sds info = genRedisInfoString(sections_dict, all_sections, everything);
6279    addReplyVerbatim(c,info,sdslen(info),"txt");
6280    sdsfree(info);
6281    releaseInfoSectionDict(sections_dict);
6282    return;
6283}
6284
6285void monitorCommand(client *c) {
6286    if (c->flags & CLIENT_DENY_BLOCKING) {
6287        /**
6288         * A client that has CLIENT_DENY_BLOCKING flag on
6289         * expects a reply per command and so can't execute MONITOR. */
6290        addReplyError(c, "MONITOR isn't allowed for DENY BLOCKING client");
6291        return;
6292    }
6293
6294    /* ignore MONITOR if already slave or in monitor mode */
6295    if (c->flags & CLIENT_SLAVE) return;
6296
6297    c->flags |= (CLIENT_SLAVE|CLIENT_MONITOR);
6298    listAddNodeTail(server.monitors,c);
6299    addReply(c,shared.ok);
6300}
6301
6302/* =================================== Main! ================================ */
6303
6304int checkIgnoreWarning(const char *warning) {
6305    int argc, j;
6306    sds *argv = sdssplitargs(server.ignore_warnings, &argc);
6307    if (argv == NULL)
6308        return 0;
6309
6310    for (j = 0; j < argc; j++) {
6311        char *flag = argv[j];
6312        if (!strcasecmp(flag, warning))
6313            break;
6314    }
6315    sdsfreesplitres(argv,argc);
6316    return j < argc;
6317}
6318
6319#ifdef __linux__
6320#include <sys/prctl.h>
6321/* since linux-3.5, kernel supports to set the state of the "THP disable" flag
6322 * for the calling thread. PR_SET_THP_DISABLE is defined in linux/prctl.h */
6323static int THPDisable(void) {
6324    int ret = -EINVAL;
6325
6326    if (!server.disable_thp)
6327        return ret;
6328
6329#ifdef PR_SET_THP_DISABLE
6330    ret = prctl(PR_SET_THP_DISABLE, 1, 0, 0, 0);
6331#endif
6332
6333    return ret;
6334}
6335
6336void linuxMemoryWarnings(void) {
6337    sds err_msg = NULL;
6338    if (checkOvercommit(&err_msg) < 0) {
6339        serverLog(LL_WARNING,"WARNING %s", err_msg);
6340        sdsfree(err_msg);
6341    }
6342    if (checkTHPEnabled(&err_msg) < 0) {
6343        server.thp_enabled = 1;
6344        if (THPDisable() == 0) {
6345            server.thp_enabled = 0;
6346        } else {
6347            serverLog(LL_WARNING, "WARNING %s", err_msg);
6348        }
6349        sdsfree(err_msg);
6350    }
6351}
6352#endif /* __linux__ */
6353
6354void createPidFile(void) {
6355    /* If pidfile requested, but no pidfile defined, use
6356     * default pidfile path */
6357    if (!server.pidfile) server.pidfile = zstrdup(CONFIG_DEFAULT_PID_FILE);
6358
6359    /* Try to write the pid file in a best-effort way. */
6360    FILE *fp = fopen(server.pidfile,"w");
6361    if (fp) {
6362        fprintf(fp,"%d\n",(int)getpid());
6363        fclose(fp);
6364    } else {
6365        serverLog(LL_WARNING, "Failed to write PID file: %s", strerror(errno));
6366    }
6367}
6368
6369void daemonize(void) {
6370    int fd;
6371
6372    if (fork() != 0) exit(0); /* parent exits */
6373    setsid(); /* create a new session */
6374
6375    /* Every output goes to /dev/null. If Redis is daemonized but
6376     * the 'logfile' is set to 'stdout' in the configuration file
6377     * it will not log at all. */
6378    if ((fd = open("/dev/null", O_RDWR, 0)) != -1) {
6379        dup2(fd, STDIN_FILENO);
6380        dup2(fd, STDOUT_FILENO);
6381        dup2(fd, STDERR_FILENO);
6382        if (fd > STDERR_FILENO) close(fd);
6383    }
6384}
6385
6386void version(void) {
6387    printf("Redis server v=%s sha=%s:%d malloc=%s bits=%d build=%llx\n",
6388        REDIS_VERSION,
6389        redisGitSHA1(),
6390        atoi(redisGitDirty()) > 0,
6391        ZMALLOC_LIB,
6392        sizeof(long) == 4 ? 32 : 64,
6393        (unsigned long long) redisBuildId());
6394    exit(0);
6395}
6396
6397void usage(void) {
6398    fprintf(stderr,"Usage: ./redis-server [/path/to/redis.conf] [options] [-]\n");
6399    fprintf(stderr,"       ./redis-server - (read config from stdin)\n");
6400    fprintf(stderr,"       ./redis-server -v or --version\n");
6401    fprintf(stderr,"       ./redis-server -h or --help\n");
6402    fprintf(stderr,"       ./redis-server --test-memory <megabytes>\n");
6403    fprintf(stderr,"       ./redis-server --check-system\n");
6404    fprintf(stderr,"\n");
6405    fprintf(stderr,"Examples:\n");
6406    fprintf(stderr,"       ./redis-server (run the server with default conf)\n");
6407    fprintf(stderr,"       echo 'maxmemory 128mb' | ./redis-server -\n");
6408    fprintf(stderr,"       ./redis-server /etc/redis/6379.conf\n");
6409    fprintf(stderr,"       ./redis-server --port 7777\n");
6410    fprintf(stderr,"       ./redis-server --port 7777 --replicaof 127.0.0.1 8888\n");
6411    fprintf(stderr,"       ./redis-server /etc/myredis.conf --loglevel verbose -\n");
6412    fprintf(stderr,"       ./redis-server /etc/myredis.conf --loglevel verbose\n\n");
6413    fprintf(stderr,"Sentinel mode:\n");
6414    fprintf(stderr,"       ./redis-server /etc/sentinel.conf --sentinel\n");
6415    exit(1);
6416}
6417
6418void redisAsciiArt(void) {
6419#include "asciilogo.h"
6420    char *buf = zmalloc(1024*16);
6421    char *mode;
6422
6423    if (server.cluster_enabled) mode = "cluster";
6424    else if (server.sentinel_mode) mode = "sentinel";
6425    else mode = "standalone";
6426
6427    /* Show the ASCII logo if: log file is stdout AND stdout is a
6428     * tty AND syslog logging is disabled. Also show logo if the user
6429     * forced us to do so via redis.conf. */
6430    int show_logo = ((!server.syslog_enabled &&
6431                      server.logfile[0] == '\0' &&
6432                      isatty(fileno(stdout))) ||
6433                     server.always_show_logo);
6434
6435    if (!show_logo) {
6436        serverLog(LL_NOTICE,
6437            "Running mode=%s, port=%d.",
6438            mode, server.port ? server.port : server.tls_port
6439        );
6440    } else {
6441        snprintf(buf,1024*16,ascii_logo,
6442            REDIS_VERSION,
6443            redisGitSHA1(),
6444            strtol(redisGitDirty(),NULL,10) > 0,
6445            (sizeof(long) == 8) ? "64" : "32",
6446            mode, server.port ? server.port : server.tls_port,
6447            (long) getpid()
6448        );
6449        serverLogRaw(LL_NOTICE|LL_RAW,buf);
6450    }
6451    zfree(buf);
6452}
6453
6454/* Get the server listener by type name */
6455connListener *listenerByType(const char *typename) {
6456    int conn_index;
6457
6458    conn_index = connectionIndexByType(typename);
6459    if (conn_index < 0)
6460        return NULL;
6461
6462    return &server.listeners[conn_index];
6463}
6464
6465/* Close original listener, re-create a new listener from the updated bind address & port */
6466int changeListener(connListener *listener) {
6467    /* Close old servers */
6468    closeListener(listener);
6469
6470    /* Just close the server if port disabled */
6471    if (listener->port == 0) {
6472        if (server.set_proc_title) redisSetProcTitle(NULL);
6473        return C_OK;
6474    }
6475
6476    /* Re-create listener */
6477    if (connListen(listener) != C_OK) {
6478        return C_ERR;
6479    }
6480
6481    /* Create event handlers */
6482    if (createSocketAcceptHandler(listener, listener->ct->accept_handler) != C_OK) {
6483        serverPanic("Unrecoverable error creating %s accept handler.", listener->ct->get_type(NULL));
6484    }
6485
6486    if (server.set_proc_title) redisSetProcTitle(NULL);
6487
6488    return C_OK;
6489}
6490
6491static void sigShutdownHandler(int sig) {
6492    char *msg;
6493
6494    switch (sig) {
6495    case SIGINT:
6496        msg = "Received SIGINT scheduling shutdown...";
6497        break;
6498    case SIGTERM:
6499        msg = "Received SIGTERM scheduling shutdown...";
6500        break;
6501    default:
6502        msg = "Received shutdown signal, scheduling shutdown...";
6503    };
6504
6505    /* SIGINT is often delivered via Ctrl+C in an interactive session.
6506     * If we receive the signal the second time, we interpret this as
6507     * the user really wanting to quit ASAP without waiting to persist
6508     * on disk and without waiting for lagging replicas. */
6509    if (server.shutdown_asap && sig == SIGINT) {
6510        serverLogFromHandler(LL_WARNING, "You insist... exiting now.");
6511        rdbRemoveTempFile(getpid(), 1);
6512        exit(1); /* Exit with an error since this was not a clean shutdown. */
6513    } else if (server.loading) {
6514        msg = "Received shutdown signal during loading, scheduling shutdown.";
6515    }
6516
6517    serverLogFromHandler(LL_WARNING, msg);
6518    server.shutdown_asap = 1;
6519    server.last_sig_received = sig;
6520}
6521
6522void setupSignalHandlers(void) {
6523    struct sigaction act;
6524
6525    /* When the SA_SIGINFO flag is set in sa_flags then sa_sigaction is used.
6526     * Otherwise, sa_handler is used. */
6527    sigemptyset(&act.sa_mask);
6528    act.sa_flags = 0;
6529    act.sa_handler = sigShutdownHandler;
6530    sigaction(SIGTERM, &act, NULL);
6531    sigaction(SIGINT, &act, NULL);
6532
6533    sigemptyset(&act.sa_mask);
6534    act.sa_flags = SA_NODEFER | SA_RESETHAND | SA_SIGINFO;
6535    act.sa_sigaction = sigsegvHandler;
6536    if(server.crashlog_enabled) {
6537        sigaction(SIGSEGV, &act, NULL);
6538        sigaction(SIGBUS, &act, NULL);
6539        sigaction(SIGFPE, &act, NULL);
6540        sigaction(SIGILL, &act, NULL);
6541        sigaction(SIGABRT, &act, NULL);
6542    }
6543    return;
6544}
6545
6546void removeSignalHandlers(void) {
6547    struct sigaction act;
6548    sigemptyset(&act.sa_mask);
6549    act.sa_flags = SA_NODEFER | SA_RESETHAND;
6550    act.sa_handler = SIG_DFL;
6551    sigaction(SIGSEGV, &act, NULL);
6552    sigaction(SIGBUS, &act, NULL);
6553    sigaction(SIGFPE, &act, NULL);
6554    sigaction(SIGILL, &act, NULL);
6555    sigaction(SIGABRT, &act, NULL);
6556}
6557
6558/* This is the signal handler for children process. It is currently useful
6559 * in order to track the SIGUSR1, that we send to a child in order to terminate
6560 * it in a clean way, without the parent detecting an error and stop
6561 * accepting writes because of a write error condition. */
6562static void sigKillChildHandler(int sig) {
6563    UNUSED(sig);
6564    int level = server.in_fork_child == CHILD_TYPE_MODULE? LL_VERBOSE: LL_WARNING;
6565    serverLogFromHandler(level, "Received SIGUSR1 in child, exiting now.");
6566    exitFromChild(SERVER_CHILD_NOERROR_RETVAL);
6567}
6568
6569void setupChildSignalHandlers(void) {
6570    struct sigaction act;
6571
6572    /* When the SA_SIGINFO flag is set in sa_flags then sa_sigaction is used.
6573     * Otherwise, sa_handler is used. */
6574    sigemptyset(&act.sa_mask);
6575    act.sa_flags = 0;
6576    act.sa_handler = sigKillChildHandler;
6577    sigaction(SIGUSR1, &act, NULL);
6578}
6579
6580/* After fork, the child process will inherit the resources
6581 * of the parent process, e.g. fd(socket or flock) etc.
6582 * should close the resources not used by the child process, so that if the
6583 * parent restarts it can bind/lock despite the child possibly still running. */
6584void closeChildUnusedResourceAfterFork(void) {
6585    closeListeningSockets(0);
6586    if (server.cluster_enabled && server.cluster_config_file_lock_fd != -1)
6587        close(server.cluster_config_file_lock_fd);  /* don't care if this fails */
6588
6589    /* Clear server.pidfile, this is the parent pidfile which should not
6590     * be touched (or deleted) by the child (on exit / crash) */
6591    zfree(server.pidfile);
6592    server.pidfile = NULL;
6593}
6594
6595/* purpose is one of CHILD_TYPE_ types */
6596int redisFork(int purpose) {
6597    if (isMutuallyExclusiveChildType(purpose)) {
6598        if (hasActiveChildProcess()) {
6599            errno = EEXIST;
6600            return -1;
6601        }
6602
6603        openChildInfoPipe();
6604    }
6605
6606    int childpid;
6607    long long start = ustime();
6608    if ((childpid = fork()) == 0) {
6609        /* Child.
6610         *
6611         * The order of setting things up follows some reasoning:
6612         * Setup signal handlers first because a signal could fire at any time.
6613         * Adjust OOM score before everything else to assist the OOM killer if
6614         * memory resources are low.
6615         */
6616        server.in_fork_child = purpose;
6617        setupChildSignalHandlers();
6618        setOOMScoreAdj(CONFIG_OOM_BGCHILD);
6619        updateDictResizePolicy();
6620        dismissMemoryInChild();
6621        closeChildUnusedResourceAfterFork();
6622        /* Close the reading part, so that if the parent crashes, the child will
6623         * get a write error and exit. */
6624        if (server.child_info_pipe[0] != -1)
6625            close(server.child_info_pipe[0]);
6626    } else {
6627        /* Parent */
6628        if (childpid == -1) {
6629            int fork_errno = errno;
6630            if (isMutuallyExclusiveChildType(purpose)) closeChildInfoPipe();
6631            errno = fork_errno;
6632            return -1;
6633        }
6634
6635        server.stat_total_forks++;
6636        server.stat_fork_time = ustime()-start;
6637        server.stat_fork_rate = (double) zmalloc_used_memory() * 1000000 / server.stat_fork_time / (1024*1024*1024); /* GB per second. */
6638        latencyAddSampleIfNeeded("fork",server.stat_fork_time/1000);
6639
6640        /* The child_pid and child_type are only for mutually exclusive children.
6641         * other child types should handle and store their pid's in dedicated variables.
6642         *
6643         * Today, we allows CHILD_TYPE_LDB to run in parallel with the other fork types:
6644         * - it isn't used for production, so it will not make the server be less efficient
6645         * - used for debugging, and we don't want to block it from running while other
6646         *   forks are running (like RDB and AOF) */
6647        if (isMutuallyExclusiveChildType(purpose)) {
6648            server.child_pid = childpid;
6649            server.child_type = purpose;
6650            server.stat_current_cow_peak = 0;
6651            server.stat_current_cow_bytes = 0;
6652            server.stat_current_cow_updated = 0;
6653            server.stat_current_save_keys_processed = 0;
6654            server.stat_module_progress = 0;
6655            server.stat_current_save_keys_total = dbTotalServerKeyCount();
6656        }
6657
6658        updateDictResizePolicy();
6659        moduleFireServerEvent(REDISMODULE_EVENT_FORK_CHILD,
6660                              REDISMODULE_SUBEVENT_FORK_CHILD_BORN,
6661                              NULL);
6662    }
6663    return childpid;
6664}
6665
6666void sendChildCowInfo(childInfoType info_type, char *pname) {
6667    sendChildInfoGeneric(info_type, 0, -1, pname);
6668}
6669
6670void sendChildInfo(childInfoType info_type, size_t keys, char *pname) {
6671    sendChildInfoGeneric(info_type, keys, -1, pname);
6672}
6673
6674/* Try to release pages back to the OS directly (bypassing the allocator),
6675 * in an effort to decrease CoW during fork. For small allocations, we can't
6676 * release any full page, so in an effort to avoid getting the size of the
6677 * allocation from the allocator (malloc_size) when we already know it's small,
6678 * we check the size_hint. If the size is not already known, passing a size_hint
6679 * of 0 will lead the checking the real size of the allocation.
6680 * Also please note that the size may be not accurate, so in order to make this
6681 * solution effective, the judgement for releasing memory pages should not be
6682 * too strict. */
6683void dismissMemory(void* ptr, size_t size_hint) {
6684    if (ptr == NULL) return;
6685
6686    /* madvise(MADV_DONTNEED) can not release pages if the size of memory
6687     * is too small, we try to release only for the memory which the size
6688     * is more than half of page size. */
6689    if (size_hint && size_hint <= server.page_size/2) return;
6690
6691    zmadvise_dontneed(ptr);
6692}
6693
6694/* Dismiss big chunks of memory inside a client structure, see dismissMemory() */
6695void dismissClientMemory(client *c) {
6696    /* Dismiss client query buffer and static reply buffer. */
6697    dismissMemory(c->buf, c->buf_usable_size);
6698    dismissSds(c->querybuf);
6699    /* Dismiss argv array only if we estimate it contains a big buffer. */
6700    if (c->argc && c->argv_len_sum/c->argc >= server.page_size) {
6701        for (int i = 0; i < c->argc; i++) {
6702            dismissObject(c->argv[i], 0);
6703        }
6704    }
6705    if (c->argc) dismissMemory(c->argv, c->argc*sizeof(robj*));
6706
6707    /* Dismiss the reply array only if the average buffer size is bigger
6708     * than a page. */
6709    if (listLength(c->reply) &&
6710        c->reply_bytes/listLength(c->reply) >= server.page_size)
6711    {
6712        listIter li;
6713        listNode *ln;
6714        listRewind(c->reply, &li);
6715        while ((ln = listNext(&li))) {
6716            clientReplyBlock *bulk = listNodeValue(ln);
6717            /* Default bulk size is 16k, actually it has extra data, maybe it
6718             * occupies 20k according to jemalloc bin size if using jemalloc. */
6719            if (bulk) dismissMemory(bulk, bulk->size);
6720        }
6721    }
6722}
6723
6724/* In the child process, we don't need some buffers anymore, and these are
6725 * likely to change in the parent when there's heavy write traffic.
6726 * We dismiss them right away, to avoid CoW.
6727 * see dismissMemeory(). */
6728void dismissMemoryInChild(void) {
6729    /* madvise(MADV_DONTNEED) may not work if Transparent Huge Pages is enabled. */
6730    if (server.thp_enabled) return;
6731
6732    /* Currently we use zmadvise_dontneed only when we use jemalloc with Linux.
6733     * so we avoid these pointless loops when they're not going to do anything. */
6734#if defined(USE_JEMALLOC) && defined(__linux__)
6735    listIter li;
6736    listNode *ln;
6737
6738    /* Dismiss replication buffer. We don't need to separately dismiss replication
6739     * backlog and replica' output buffer, because they just reference the global
6740     * replication buffer but don't cost real memory. */
6741    listRewind(server.repl_buffer_blocks, &li);
6742    while((ln = listNext(&li))) {
6743        replBufBlock *o = listNodeValue(ln);
6744        dismissMemory(o, o->size);
6745    }
6746
6747    /* Dismiss all clients memory. */
6748    listRewind(server.clients, &li);
6749    while((ln = listNext(&li))) {
6750        client *c = listNodeValue(ln);
6751        dismissClientMemory(c);
6752    }
6753#endif
6754}
6755
6756void memtest(size_t megabytes, int passes);

6758/* Returns 1 if there is --sentinel among the arguments or if
6759 * executable name contains "redis-sentinel". */
6760int checkForSentinelMode(int argc, char **argv, char *exec_name) {
6761    if (strstr(exec_name,"redis-sentinel") != NULL) return 1;
6762
6763    for (int j = 1; j < argc; j++)
6764        if (!strcmp(argv[j],"--sentinel")) return 1;
6765    return 0;
6766}
6767
6768/* Function called at startup to load RDB or AOF file in memory. */
6769void loadDataFromDisk(void) {
6770    long long start = ustime();
6771    if (server.aof_state == AOF_ON) {
6772        int ret = loadAppendOnlyFiles(server.aof_manifest);
6773        if (ret == AOF_FAILED || ret == AOF_OPEN_ERR)
6774            exit(1);
6775        if (ret != AOF_NOT_EXIST)
6776            serverLog(LL_NOTICE, "DB loaded from append only file: %.3f seconds", (float)(ustime()-start)/1000000);
6777    } else {
6778        rdbSaveInfo rsi = RDB_SAVE_INFO_INIT;
6779        int rsi_is_valid = 0;
6780        errno = 0; /* Prevent a stale value from affecting error checking */
6781        int rdb_flags = RDBFLAGS_NONE;
6782        if (iAmMaster()) {
6783            /* Master may delete expired keys when loading, we should
6784             * propagate expire to replication backlog. */
6785            createReplicationBacklog();
6786            rdb_flags |= RDBFLAGS_FEED_REPL;
6787        }
6788        int rdb_load_ret = rdbLoad(server.rdb_filename, &rsi, rdb_flags);
6789        if (rdb_load_ret == RDB_OK) {
6790            serverLog(LL_NOTICE,"DB loaded from disk: %.3f seconds",
6791                (float)(ustime()-start)/1000000);
6792
6793            /* Restore the replication ID / offset from the RDB file. */
6794            if (rsi.repl_id_is_set &&
6795                rsi.repl_offset != -1 &&
6796                /* Note that older implementations may save a repl_stream_db
6797                 * of -1 inside the RDB file in a wrong way, see more
6798                 * information in function rdbPopulateSaveInfo. */
6799                rsi.repl_stream_db != -1)
6800            {
6801                rsi_is_valid = 1;
6802                if (!iAmMaster()) {
6803                    memcpy(server.replid,rsi.repl_id,sizeof(server.replid));
6804                    server.master_repl_offset = rsi.repl_offset;
6805                    /* If this is a replica, create a cached master from this
6806                     * information, in order to allow partial resynchronizations
6807                     * with masters. */
6808                    replicationCacheMasterUsingMyself();
6809                    selectDb(server.cached_master,rsi.repl_stream_db);
6810                } else {
6811                    /* If this is a master, we can save the replication info
6812                     * as secondary ID and offset, in order to allow replicas
6813                     * to partial resynchronizations with masters. */
6814                    memcpy(server.replid2,rsi.repl_id,sizeof(server.replid));
6815                    server.second_replid_offset = rsi.repl_offset+1;
6816                    /* Rebase master_repl_offset from rsi.repl_offset. */
6817                    server.master_repl_offset += rsi.repl_offset;
6818                    serverAssert(server.repl_backlog);
6819                    server.repl_backlog->offset = server.master_repl_offset -
6820                              server.repl_backlog->histlen + 1;
6821                    rebaseReplicationBuffer(rsi.repl_offset);
6822                    server.repl_no_slaves_since = time(NULL);
6823                }
6824            }
6825        } else if (rdb_load_ret != RDB_NOT_EXIST) {
6826            serverLog(LL_WARNING, "Fatal error loading the DB, check server logs. Exiting.");
6827            exit(1);
6828        }
6829
6830        /* We always create replication backlog if server is a master, we need
6831         * it because we put DELs in it when loading expired keys in RDB, but
6832         * if RDB doesn't have replication info or there is no rdb, it is not
6833         * possible to support partial resynchronization, to avoid extra memory
6834         * of replication backlog, we drop it. */
6835        if (!rsi_is_valid && server.repl_backlog)
6836            freeReplicationBacklog();
6837    }
6838}
6839
6840void redisOutOfMemoryHandler(size_t allocation_size) {
6841    serverLog(LL_WARNING,"Out Of Memory allocating %zu bytes!",
6842        allocation_size);
6843    serverPanic("Redis aborting for OUT OF MEMORY. Allocating %zu bytes!",
6844        allocation_size);
6845}
6846
6847/* Callback for sdstemplate on proc-title-template. See redis.conf for
6848 * supported variables.
6849 */
6850static sds redisProcTitleGetVariable(const sds varname, void *arg)
6851{
6852    if (!strcmp(varname, "title")) {
6853        return sdsnew(arg);
6854    } else if (!strcmp(varname, "listen-addr")) {
6855        if (server.port || server.tls_port)
6856            return sdscatprintf(sdsempty(), "%s:%u",
6857                                server.bindaddr_count ? server.bindaddr[0] : "*",
6858                                server.port ? server.port : server.tls_port);
6859        else
6860            return sdscatprintf(sdsempty(), "unixsocket:%s", server.unixsocket);
6861    } else if (!strcmp(varname, "server-mode")) {
6862        if (server.cluster_enabled) return sdsnew("[cluster]");
6863        else if (server.sentinel_mode) return sdsnew("[sentinel]");
6864        else return sdsempty();
6865    } else if (!strcmp(varname, "config-file")) {
6866        return sdsnew(server.configfile ? server.configfile : "-");
6867    } else if (!strcmp(varname, "port")) {
6868        return sdscatprintf(sdsempty(), "%u", server.port);
6869    } else if (!strcmp(varname, "tls-port")) {
6870        return sdscatprintf(sdsempty(), "%u", server.tls_port);
6871    } else if (!strcmp(varname, "unixsocket")) {
6872        return sdsnew(server.unixsocket);
6873    } else
6874        return NULL;    /* Unknown variable name */
6875}
6876
6877/* Expand the specified proc-title-template string and return a newly
6878 * allocated sds, or NULL. */
6879static sds expandProcTitleTemplate(const char *template, const char *title) {
6880    sds res = sdstemplate(template, redisProcTitleGetVariable, (void *) title);
6881    if (!res)
6882        return NULL;
6883    return sdstrim(res, " ");
6884}
6885/* Validate the specified template, returns 1 if valid or 0 otherwise. */
6886int validateProcTitleTemplate(const char *template) {
6887    int ok = 1;
6888    sds res = expandProcTitleTemplate(template, "");
6889    if (!res)
6890        return 0;
6891    if (sdslen(res) == 0) ok = 0;
6892    sdsfree(res);
6893    return ok;
6894}
6895
6896int redisSetProcTitle(char *title) {
6897#ifdef USE_SETPROCTITLE
6898    if (!title) title = server.exec_argv[0];
6899    sds proc_title = expandProcTitleTemplate(server.proc_title_template, title);
6900    if (!proc_title) return C_ERR;  /* Not likely, proc_title_template is validated */
6901
6902    setproctitle("%s", proc_title);
6903    sdsfree(proc_title);
6904#else
6905    UNUSED(title);
6906#endif
6907
6908    return C_OK;
6909}

6911void redisSetCpuAffinity(const char *cpulist) {
6912#ifdef USE_SETCPUAFFINITY
6913    setcpuaffinity(cpulist);
6914#else
6915    UNUSED(cpulist);
6916#endif
6917}
6918
6919/* Send a notify message to systemd. Returns sd_notify return code which is
6920 * a positive number on success. */
6921int redisCommunicateSystemd(const char *sd_notify_msg) {
6922#ifdef HAVE_LIBSYSTEMD
6923    int ret = sd_notify(0, sd_notify_msg);
6924
6925    if (ret == 0)
6926        serverLog(LL_WARNING, "systemd supervision error: NOTIFY_SOCKET not found!");
6927    else if (ret < 0)
6928        serverLog(LL_WARNING, "systemd supervision error: sd_notify: %d", ret);
6929    return ret;
6930#else
6931    UNUSED(sd_notify_msg);
6932    return 0;
6933#endif
6934}
6935
6936/* Attempt to set up upstart supervision. Returns 1 if successful. */
6937static int redisSupervisedUpstart(void) {
6938    const char *upstart_job = getenv("UPSTART_JOB");
6939
6940    if (!upstart_job) {
6941        serverLog(LL_WARNING,
6942                "upstart supervision requested, but UPSTART_JOB not found!");
6943        return 0;
6944    }
6945
6946    serverLog(LL_NOTICE, "supervised by upstart, will stop to signal readiness.");
6947    raise(SIGSTOP);
6948    unsetenv("UPSTART_JOB");
6949    return 1;
6950}
6951
6952/* Attempt to set up systemd supervision. Returns 1 if successful. */
6953static int redisSupervisedSystemd(void) {
6954#ifndef HAVE_LIBSYSTEMD
6955    serverLog(LL_WARNING,
6956            "systemd supervision requested or auto-detected, but Redis is compiled without libsystemd support!");
6957    return 0;
6958#else
6959    if (redisCommunicateSystemd("STATUS=Redis is loading...\n") <= 0)
6960        return 0;
6961    serverLog(LL_NOTICE,
6962        "Supervised by systemd. Please make sure you set appropriate values for TimeoutStartSec and TimeoutStopSec in your service unit.");
6963    return 1;
6964#endif
6965}
6966
6967int redisIsSupervised(int mode) {
6968    int ret = 0;
6969
6970    if (mode == SUPERVISED_AUTODETECT) {
6971        if (getenv("UPSTART_JOB")) {
6972            serverLog(LL_VERBOSE, "Upstart supervision detected.");
6973            mode = SUPERVISED_UPSTART;
6974        } else if (getenv("NOTIFY_SOCKET")) {
6975            serverLog(LL_VERBOSE, "Systemd supervision detected.");
6976            mode = SUPERVISED_SYSTEMD;
6977        }
6978    }
6979
6980    switch (mode) {
6981        case SUPERVISED_UPSTART:
6982            ret = redisSupervisedUpstart();
6983            break;
6984        case SUPERVISED_SYSTEMD:
6985            ret = redisSupervisedSystemd();
6986            break;
6987        default:
6988            break;
6989    }
6990
6991    if (ret)
6992        server.supervised_mode = mode;
6993
6994    return ret;
6995}
6996
6997int iAmMaster(void) {
6998    return ((!server.cluster_enabled && server.masterhost == NULL) ||
6999            (server.cluster_enabled && nodeIsMaster(server.cluster->myself)));
7000}
