7029    {"listpack", listpackTest}
7030};
7031redisTestProc *getTestProcByName(const char *name) {
7032    int numtests = sizeof(redisTests)/sizeof(struct redisTest);
7033    for (int j = 0; j < numtests; j++) {
7034        if (!strcasecmp(name,redisTests[j].name)) {
7035            return redisTests[j].proc;
7036        }
7037    }
7038    return NULL;
7039}
7040#endif
7041
7042int main(int argc, char **argv) {
7043    struct timeval tv;
7044    int j;
7045    char config_from_stdin = 0;
7046
7047#ifdef REDIS_TEST
7048    if (argc >= 3 && !strcasecmp(argv[1], "test")) {
7049        int flags = 0;
7050        for (j = 3; j < argc; j++) {
7051            char *arg = argv[j];
7052            if (!strcasecmp(arg, "--accurate")) flags |= REDIS_TEST_ACCURATE;
7053            else if (!strcasecmp(arg, "--large-memory")) flags |= REDIS_TEST_LARGE_MEMORY;
7054            else if (!strcasecmp(arg, "--valgrind")) flags |= REDIS_TEST_VALGRIND;
7055        }
7056
7057        if (!strcasecmp(argv[2], "all")) {
7058            int numtests = sizeof(redisTests)/sizeof(struct redisTest);
7059            for (j = 0; j < numtests; j++) {
7060                redisTests[j].failed = (redisTests[j].proc(argc,argv,flags) != 0);
7061            }
7062
7063            /* Report tests result */
7064            int failed_num = 0;
7065            for (j = 0; j < numtests; j++) {
7066                if (redisTests[j].failed) {
7067                    failed_num++;
7068                    printf("[failed] Test - %s\n", redisTests[j].name);
7069                } else {
7070                    printf("[ok] Test - %s\n", redisTests[j].name);
7071                }
7072            }
7073
7074            printf("%d tests, %d passed, %d failed\n", numtests,
7075                   numtests-failed_num, failed_num);
7076
7077            return failed_num == 0 ? 0 : 1;
7078        } else {
7079            redisTestProc *proc = getTestProcByName(argv[2]);
7080            if (!proc) return -1; /* test not found */
7081            return proc(argc,argv,flags);
7082        }
7083
7084        return 0;
7085    }
7086#endif
7087
7088    /* We need to initialize our libraries, and the server configuration. */
7089#ifdef INIT_SETPROCTITLE_REPLACEMENT
7090    spt_init(argc, argv);
7091#endif
7092    tzset(); /* Populates 'timezone' global. */
7093    zmalloc_set_oom_handler(redisOutOfMemoryHandler);
7094
7095    /* To achieve entropy, in case of containers, their time() and getpid() can
7096     * be the same. But value of tv_usec is fast enough to make the difference */
7097    gettimeofday(&tv,NULL);
7098    srand(time(NULL)^getpid()^tv.tv_usec);
7099    srandom(time(NULL)^getpid()^tv.tv_usec);
7100    init_genrand64(((long long) tv.tv_sec * 1000000 + tv.tv_usec) ^ getpid());
7101    crc64_init();
7102
7103    /* Store umask value. Because umask(2) only offers a set-and-get API we have
7104     * to reset it and restore it back. We do this early to avoid a potential
7105     * race condition with threads that could be creating files or directories.
7106     */
7107    umask(server.umask = umask(0777));
7108
7109    uint8_t hashseed[16];
7110    getRandomBytes(hashseed,sizeof(hashseed));
7111    dictSetHashFunctionSeed(hashseed);
7112
7113    char *exec_name = strrchr(argv[0], '/');
7114    if (exec_name == NULL) exec_name = argv[0];
7115    server.sentinel_mode = checkForSentinelMode(argc,argv, exec_name);
7116    initServerConfig();
7117    ACLInit(); /* The ACL subsystem must be initialized ASAP because the
7118                  basic networking code and client creation depends on it. */
7119    moduleInitModulesSystem();
7120    connTypeInitialize();
7121
7122    /* Store the executable path and arguments in a safe place in order
7123     * to be able to restart the server later. */
7124    server.executable = getAbsolutePath(argv[0]);
7125    server.exec_argv = zmalloc(sizeof(char*)*(argc+1));
7126    server.exec_argv[argc] = NULL;
7127    for (j = 0; j < argc; j++) server.exec_argv[j] = zstrdup(argv[j]);
7128
7129    /* We need to init sentinel right now as parsing the configuration file
7130     * in sentinel mode will have the effect of populating the sentinel
7131     * data structures with master nodes to monitor. */
7132    if (server.sentinel_mode) {
7133        initSentinelConfig();
7134        initSentinel();
7135    }
7136
7137    /* Check if we need to start in redis-check-rdb/aof mode. We just execute
7138     * the program main. However the program is part of the Redis executable
7139     * so that we can easily execute an RDB check on loading errors. */
7140    if (strstr(exec_name,"redis-check-rdb") != NULL)
7141        redis_check_rdb_main(argc,argv,NULL);
7142    else if (strstr(exec_name,"redis-check-aof") != NULL)
7143        redis_check_aof_main(argc,argv);
7144
7145    if (argc >= 2) {
7146        j = 1; /* First option to parse in argv[] */
7147        sds options = sdsempty();
7148
7149        /* Handle special options --help and --version */
7150        if (strcmp(argv[1], "-v") == 0 ||
7151            strcmp(argv[1], "--version") == 0) version();
7152        if (strcmp(argv[1], "--help") == 0 ||
7153            strcmp(argv[1], "-h") == 0) usage();
7154        if (strcmp(argv[1], "--test-memory") == 0) {
7155            if (argc == 3) {
7156                memtest(atoi(argv[2]),50);
7157                exit(0);
7158            } else {
7159                fprintf(stderr,"Please specify the amount of memory to test in megabytes.\n");
7160                fprintf(stderr,"Example: ./redis-server --test-memory 4096\n\n");
7161                exit(1);
7162            }
7163        } if (strcmp(argv[1], "--check-system") == 0) {
7164            exit(syscheck() ? 0 : 1);
7165        }
7166        /* Parse command line options
7167         * Precedence wise, File, stdin, explicit options -- last config is the one that matters.
7168         *
7169         * First argument is the config file name? */
7170        if (argv[1][0] != '-') {
7171            /* Replace the config file in server.exec_argv with its absolute path. */
7172            server.configfile = getAbsolutePath(argv[1]);
7173            zfree(server.exec_argv[1]);
7174            server.exec_argv[1] = zstrdup(server.configfile);
7175            j = 2; // Skip this arg when parsing options
7176        }
7177        sds *argv_tmp;
7178        int argc_tmp;
7179        int handled_last_config_arg = 1;
7180        while(j < argc) {
7181            /* Either first or last argument - Should we read config from stdin? */
7182            if (argv[j][0] == '-' && argv[j][1] == '\0' && (j == 1 || j == argc-1)) {
7183                config_from_stdin = 1;
7184            }
7185            /* All the other options are parsed and conceptually appended to the
7186             * configuration file. For instance --port 6380 will generate the
7187             * string "port 6380\n" to be parsed after the actual config file
7188             * and stdin input are parsed (if they exist).
7189             * Only consider that if the last config has at least one argument. */
7190            else if (handled_last_config_arg && argv[j][0] == '-' && argv[j][1] == '-') {
7191                /* Option name */
7192                if (sdslen(options)) options = sdscat(options,"\n");
7193                /* argv[j]+2 for removing the preceding `--` */
7194                options = sdscat(options,argv[j]+2);
7195                options = sdscat(options," ");
7196
7197                argv_tmp = sdssplitargs(argv[j], &argc_tmp);
7198                if (argc_tmp == 1) {
7199                    /* Means that we only have one option name, like --port or "--port " */
7200                    handled_last_config_arg = 0;
7201
7202                    if ((j != argc-1) && argv[j+1][0] == '-' && argv[j+1][1] == '-' &&
7203                        !strcasecmp(argv[j], "--save"))
7204                    {
7205                        /* Special case: handle some things like `--save --config value`.
7206                         * In this case, if next argument starts with `--`, we will reset
7207                         * handled_last_config_arg flag and append an empty "" config value
7208                         * to the options, so it will become `--save "" --config value`.
7209                         * We are doing it to be compatible with pre 7.0 behavior (which we
7210                         * break it in #10660, 7.0.1), since there might be users who generate
7211                         * a command line from an array and when it's empty that's what they produce. */
7212                        options = sdscat(options, "\"\"");
7213                        handled_last_config_arg = 1;
7214                    }
7215                    else if ((j == argc-1) && !strcasecmp(argv[j], "--save")) {
7216                        /* Special case: when empty save is the last argument.
7217                         * In this case, we append an empty "" config value to the options,
7218                         * so it will become `--save ""` and will follow the same reset thing. */
7219                        options = sdscat(options, "\"\"");
7220                    }
7221                    else if ((j != argc-1) && argv[j+1][0] == '-' && argv[j+1][1] == '-' &&
7222                        !strcasecmp(argv[j], "--sentinel"))
7223                    {
7224                        /* Special case: handle some things like `--sentinel --config value`.
7225                         * It is a pseudo config option with no value. In this case, if next
7226                         * argument starts with `--`, we will reset handled_last_config_arg flag.
7227                         * We are doing it to be compatible with pre 7.0 behavior (which we
7228                         * break it in #10660, 7.0.1). */
7229                        options = sdscat(options, "");
7230                        handled_last_config_arg = 1;
7231                    }
7232                    else if ((j == argc-1) && !strcasecmp(argv[j], "--sentinel")) {
7233                        /* Special case: when --sentinel is the last argument.
7234                         * It is a pseudo config option with no value. In this case, do nothing.
7235                         * We are doing it to be compatible with pre 7.0 behavior (which we
7236                         * break it in #10660, 7.0.1). */
7237                        options = sdscat(options, "");
7238                    }
7239                } else {
7240                    /* Means that we are passing both config name and it's value in the same arg,
7241                     * like "--port 6380", so we need to reset handled_last_config_arg flag. */
7242                    handled_last_config_arg = 1;
7243                }
7244                sdsfreesplitres(argv_tmp, argc_tmp);
7245            } else {
7246                /* Option argument */
7247                options = sdscatrepr(options,argv[j],strlen(argv[j]));
7248                options = sdscat(options," ");
7249                handled_last_config_arg = 1;
7250            }
7251            j++;
7252        }
7253
7254        loadServerConfig(server.configfile, config_from_stdin, options);
7255        if (server.sentinel_mode) loadSentinelConfigFromQueue();
7256        sdsfree(options);
7257    }
7258    if (server.sentinel_mode) sentinelCheckConfigFile();
7259
7260    /* Do system checks */
7261#ifdef __linux__
7262    linuxMemoryWarnings();
7263    sds err_msg = NULL;
7264    if (checkXenClocksource(&err_msg) < 0) {
7265        serverLog(LL_WARNING, "WARNING %s", err_msg);
7266        sdsfree(err_msg);
7267    }
7268#if defined (__arm64__)
7269    int ret;
7270    if ((ret = checkLinuxMadvFreeForkBug(&err_msg)) <= 0) {
7271        if (ret < 0) {
7272            serverLog(LL_WARNING, "WARNING %s", err_msg);
7273            sdsfree(err_msg);
7274        } else
7275            serverLog(LL_WARNING, "Failed to test the kernel for a bug that could lead to data corruption during background save. "
7276                                  "Your system could be affected, please report this error.");
7277        if (!checkIgnoreWarning("ARM64-COW-BUG")) {
7278            serverLog(LL_WARNING,"Redis will now exit to prevent data corruption. "
7279                                 "Note that it is possible to suppress this warning by setting the following config: ignore-warnings ARM64-COW-BUG");
7280            exit(1);
7281        }
7282    }
7283#endif /* __arm64__ */
7284#endif /* __linux__ */
7285
7286    /* Daemonize if needed */
7287    server.supervised = redisIsSupervised(server.supervised_mode);
7288    int background = server.daemonize && !server.supervised;
7289    if (background) daemonize();
7290
7291    serverLog(LL_NOTICE, "oO0OoO0OoO0Oo Redis is starting oO0OoO0OoO0Oo");
7292    serverLog(LL_NOTICE,
7293        "Redis version=%s, bits=%d, commit=%s, modified=%d, pid=%d, just started",
7294            REDIS_VERSION,
7295            (sizeof(long) == 8) ? 64 : 32,
7296            redisGitSHA1(),
7297            strtol(redisGitDirty(),NULL,10) > 0,
7298            (int)getpid());
7299
7300    if (argc == 1) {
7301        serverLog(LL_WARNING, "Warning: no config file specified, using the default config. In order to specify a config file use %s /path/to/redis.conf", argv[0]);
7302    } else {
7303        serverLog(LL_NOTICE, "Configuration loaded");
7304    }
7305
7306    initServer();
7307    if (background || server.pidfile) createPidFile();
7308    if (server.set_proc_title) redisSetProcTitle(NULL);
7309    redisAsciiArt();
7310    checkTcpBacklogSettings();
7311    if (server.cluster_enabled) {
7312        clusterInit();
7313    }
7314    if (!server.sentinel_mode) {
7315        moduleInitModulesSystemLast();
7316        moduleLoadFromQueue();
7317    }
7318    ACLLoadUsersAtStartup();
7319    initListeners();
7320    if (server.cluster_enabled) {
7321        clusterInitListeners();
7322    }
7323    InitServerLast();
7324
7325    if (!server.sentinel_mode) {
7326        /* Things not needed when running in Sentinel mode. */
7327        serverLog(LL_NOTICE,"Server initialized");
7328        aofLoadManifestFromDisk();
7329        loadDataFromDisk();
7330        aofOpenIfNeededOnServerStart();
7331        aofDelHistoryFiles();
7332        if (server.cluster_enabled) {
7333            serverAssert(verifyClusterConfigWithData() == C_OK);
7334        }
7335
7336        for (j = 0; j < CONN_TYPE_MAX; j++) {
7337            connListener *listener = &server.listeners[j];
7338            if (listener->ct == NULL)
7339                continue;
7340
7341            serverLog(LL_NOTICE,"Ready to accept connections %s", listener->ct->get_type(NULL));
7342        }
7343
7344        if (server.supervised_mode == SUPERVISED_SYSTEMD) {
7345            if (!server.masterhost) {
7346                redisCommunicateSystemd("STATUS=Ready to accept connections\n");
7347            } else {
7348                redisCommunicateSystemd("STATUS=Ready to accept connections in read-only mode. Waiting for MASTER <-> REPLICA sync\n");
7349            }
7350            redisCommunicateSystemd("READY=1\n");
7351        }
7352    } else {
7353        sentinelIsRunning();
7354        if (server.supervised_mode == SUPERVISED_SYSTEMD) {
7355            redisCommunicateSystemd("STATUS=Ready to accept connections\n");
7356            redisCommunicateSystemd("READY=1\n");
7357        }
7358    }
7359
7360    /* Warning the user about suspicious maxmemory setting. */
7361    if (server.maxmemory > 0 && server.maxmemory < 1024*1024) {
7362        serverLog(LL_WARNING,"WARNING: You specified a maxmemory value that is less than 1MB (current value is %llu bytes). Are you sure this is what you really want?", server.maxmemory);
7363    }
7364
7365    redisSetCpuAffinity(server.server_cpulist);
7366    setOOMScoreAdj(-1);
7367
7368    aeMain(server.el);
7369    aeDeleteEventLoop(server.el);
7370    return 0;
7371}
7372
7373/* The End */
